TID Outdoor Day Party: 09/08/2026 02:00 PM = listing. The 17 May 2026 web.archive.org copy of the home page has 12 dated rows: 11 are in the live 2026 folder, 10 of them on the same date (one start differs by an hour, same date) and one 8-week course moved from 27 Apr to 8 Jun (S1 either way); the 12th, a January 2027 row, is not in the 2026 folder. iCal DTSTART writes local wall time with a nominal Z (19:00Z = listing 07:00 PM), so the listing's local dates are used. These are show dates.
- robots.txt (16 Oct 2026): allowed. www.trinitybristol.org.uk/robots.txt: 'User-agent: * / Disallow:' (allow all); only Googlebot has its own form rules, and there is no Anthropic group. polite.py allowed every URL used; web.archive.org allowed.
- Terms (16 Oct 2026): copyright-only. The only notice is the footer on every page: 'Except where otherwise indicated, this site remains (c) 2004-2026 of Trinity Community Arts (Registered Charity Number 1144770 Registered Company Number 4372577) or contributing authors / This site was designed and built using Free Software' (a bare notice, which Amendment 14 says is not terms). The footer's 'Terms & Conditions' link (www.3ca.org.uk/contact/tickets) redirects to the trinitybristol host and was refused by polite.py as a cross-host redirect; the same ticket-sale terms at /whats-on/tickets cover purchase, resale, entry and refunds ('for personal use only' is about tickets, not site content) and say nothing on copying or reuse. The sitemap (296 links) has no terms, legal, copyright or licence page. Checked: https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/whats-on/2026 (footer); https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/whats-on/tickets; https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/contact/privacy; https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/contact/cookies; https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/accessibility-info; https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/sitemap; https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/contact/online/feeds/trinity (offers web feeds); https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/terms (404); https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/terms-and-conditions (404); https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/legal (404)
- Rooms: the iCal LOCATION field and the event page's 'Where' field ('Trinity (Main Hall)', 'Trinity (Fyfe Hall)' or 'Trinity, Fyfe Hall', 'Trinity (The Den)' or 'The Den', 'Trinity garden'). The Den and the garden are separate spaces on /booking/spaces (The Den is a seasonal outdoor venue in the grounds; the community garden is hired with an indoor space). Rows that say only 'Trinity' or 'Trinity Centre', rows with no LOCATION, and the Garden Party row (its iCal title differs only by a double space; LOCATION 'Trinity') go to the house room `brs-trinity-house` (Amendment 14). Rows whose LOCATION is another building are `off-site`: S1 1 (The Island), S2 4 (Jacobs Wells Baths 2, Queen Square 2).
- Coverage: S1 52, S2 36 on the 2026 folder (canceled: S1 2, S2 3; Amendment 4 words: S1 1 workshop row, S2 0; no start between 00:01 and 04:59). By room: house 47 / 32, The Den 3 / 3, Fyfe Hall 2 / 0, garden 0 / 1, Main Hall 0 / 0. The 2026 folder counts are exact; for the whole What's On they are lower bounds. /whats-on/2025 (read in full) holds 1 more S2 row, and the /whats-on page shows 2 S1 rows and 1 S2 row from /whats-on/workshops-classes, a folder that was not listed in full; none was checked for room (seen so far 54 / 38, not a ceiling). 9 S1/S2 rows have no LOCATION and their pages were not read (S1 3 counted, S2 6); even if every one were off-site, the venue keeps 49 / 30, and even if every one were in The Den it would have 6 / 9, so no room can reach 20 / 20. The house room is a pool room by definition and no named room reaches 20 / 20, so every room is pool (pool-only venue, Amendment 14).
- Platform: self-hosted Plone ('built using Free Software'), not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or a Rockhouse Partners site. No bot check and no 403. The 'Book Tickets' buttons on the event pages saved on disk go to hdfst.uk (Headfirst Bristol, a Bristol not-for-profit ticketing and listings service; no public source links it to Live Nation or AEG); my.3ca.org.uk, the operator's CiviCRM, carries only mailing-list and register links.
- Also checked: operator and booker is Trinity Community Arts Ltd (registered charity 1144770, company 4372577), in house ('one of the few remaining independent music venues in the UK'; /booking/gigs invites promoters to programme gigs through a form; 'trinity presents' marks its own promotions). Outside hirers and promoters present single nights; touring gigs are about 25 of the 88 counted rows, so no outside company presents more than half. The building's owner is not identified (sources differ between a Bristol City Council freehold with a lease and a transfer to the charity; the 35-year Community Asset Transfer lease found in search results is for Jacobs Wells Baths, the operator's other building). No public source names a Live Nation, AEG or Academy Music Group owner, stake or booker. Capacities from https://www.trinitybristol.org.uk/booking/spaces: Main Hall 600 standing, The Den 500 standing, Fyfe Hall 300 standing.
- §5: Bristol's only other eligible cluster is The Louisiana (`brs-louisiana`, re-admitted in the merge), so Trinity Centre passes §5 only through it. Trinity is a pool-only venue and feeds the Bristol pool.
- Requests during the eligibility check: www.trinitybristol.org.uk 29, web.archive.org 3, www.3ca.org.uk 2, trinitybristol.org.uk 1 (requests.log; includes the second check's 4 requests and the scouting CDX reads of 15 Oct)

Design by Miaso Design', a bare notice (Amendment 14). There is no terms or privacy link in the menu or footer. A scan of the visible text of all 19 saved venue-host bodies and of the archive copy found no text that forbids copying, reproducing, republishing or automated reading, or limits use to personal viewing. Checked: https://greenmilljazz.com/ (footer); https://greenmilljazz.com/faqs/; https://greenmilljazz.com/contact/; https://greenmilljazz.com/location/; https://greenmilljazz.com/media/; https://greenmilljazz.com/terms (404); https://greenmilljazz.com/terms-of-use (404); https://greenmilljazz.com/terms-of-service (404); https://greenmilljazz.com/terms-and-conditions (404); https://greenmilljazz.com/legal (404); https://greenmilljazz.com/privacy-policy/ (404)
- Platform: self-hosted WordPress (generator 'WordPress 7.1.3' live, 'WordPress 6.8.5' in the 13 May copy) with the Events Manager plugin (7.4.8 live, 7.2.3.1 in the copy) and a custom theme 'greenmill' ('Design by Miaso Design'). Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue, Weebly or Webflow, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer on any of the 19 saved venue-host bodies or the archive copy. No bot check and no 403 in front of the listings: every venue-host answer was a 200 (15) or a 404 (6). The only 'Please wait while' text in the pages is Events Manager's booking-widget label ('Please wait while the booking is being submitted.') in the page script settings. The web.archive.org calendar copy is the real page ('Calendar | Green Mill', May 2026 grid), not a check page; the archive's one 503 was on a CDX query and was retried with a 200.
- Operator (§1, Amendment 14): a private owner. The club was held by one private owner from 1986 to 19 Jun 2026 and since then by two of its longtime general managers, both private persons (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_Mill_Cocktail_Lounge; https://www.uptownupdate.com/2026/06/the-green-mill-has-new-owners.html; https://www.preservationchicago.org/win-the-green-mill-sold-to-long-time-general-managers-who-will-continue-legacy/; a Chicago Tribune column reprinted at https://www.dailygazette.com/tribune/column-the-green-mill-has-new-owners-and-the-music-plays-on/article_c3181d95-3f24-51db-a078-2a658358151d.html). No company owner is named. The venue's own home page (saved body of 16 Oct 2026 UTC) names only the private person who opened the club on 19 Jun 1986 and names no company; it does not mention the sale, which rests on the press pages listed, read by web search at the first check and its check (not saved, not in requests.log). The building was listed for sale in 2025 and its buyer is not identified; no source ties it to Live Nation or AEG. Booking is in-house: the site names no booker or promoter, its only contact is the club's general address info.greenmilljazz@gmail.com, and it sells no tickets ('Q: How can I buy tickets? A: We don't sell tickets.'; 'We take cash only.'). No public source read names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake, house booker or sale. The only company or group named as presenting in the rows is the Chicago Jazz Composers Collective, a musicians' collective whose Sunday afternoon rows say '... presents:' in their details (6 of 286 counted rows; 7 rows carry its name in the title), far below half, so it is not a house booker. Under the literal reading of Amendment 1 it is an operator of the room for the sensitivity analysis; it is neither Live Nation nor AEG and runs no other listed venue as far as the pages show. Two S2 rows (31 Jul and 1 Aug) are titled '<bandleader> presents' his own orchestra; the presenter there is a musician, a private person, not a company, so he is no operator under either reading.
- Rooms: one. The Google Calendar `location` value is empty in all 356 cell rows, the rows have no room or stage field, and the calendar has no room or location filter (only month arrows).
- §5: the Chicago metro (Chicago-Naperville-Elgin, IL-IN MSA, OMB Bulletin 23-01) has eligible venues of other clusters: Martyrs' (test, one room) and Reggies Chicago (test rooms Rock Club, Music Joint and Comedy Shack), settled 17-18 Oct 2026; Old Town School of Folk Music is pool. None shares an owner or booker with Green Mill. §6: Chicago is not in Study 1's Minneapolis-St Paul metro, and Green Mill is neither Hideout nor Black Cat (Amendment 11).
- Coverage: S1 >=129 (>=135 with the 13 May copy), S2 >=157, both lower bounds. Live grids: 289 rows on nights 1 Apr-30 Sep (S1 130, S2 159), on every one of the 183 nights, at most 3 per night (26 nights have 3) and with no 'more' link. Removed: S1 1 row, '(all day) CLOSED FOR A PRIVATE PARTY' on 30 Jun (Amendment 4 word 'closed'; it replaced that night's listing); S2 1 row with an empty title (16 Aug) and 1 notice that a show will not happen ('There will be no Paper Machete July 4th', dropped as canceled). Counted on the live grids: S1 129, S2 157; by month Apr 41, May 43, Jun 45, Jul 50, Aug 55, Sep 52. The 13 May copy holds 6 rows that the live grid no longer shows, with no cancel mark: the Friday 5 pm residency on 1, 8, 15, 22 and 29 May and 5 Jun (the live grid shows that residency only from 7 Aug, under a new event); with them S1 is 135. Every other row of the copy is on the live grid (some retitled). So the live grid drops past rows when a recurring event is replaced, and both periods are lower bounds; no copy of the Jul-Sep grids was read. On 28 Jun the Chicago Jazz Composers Collective is listed twice at 16:00 under two event pages; both count in S1 (Amendment 12 folds repeats in S2 only), 128 (134) if folded. No S2 night lists the same title twice. The only Amendment 4 word in any row is 'closed'. Spoken-word rows (Paper Machete 25, Uptown Poetry Slam 6) carry no Amendment 4 word and count; the rest are music nights, mostly weekly jazz residencies. Test room.
- Coverage, compiled (18 Oct 2026, Amendment 12): the compiled `shows.csv` (295 rows: 289 from the six live month grids and 6 from the 13 May copy) gives S1 135, S2 157 for the one room (written rows 136 / 159). The compile re-read the six live month grids on 18 Oct (6 requests to greenmilljazz.com through polite.py, all 200; web.archive.org 0); polite.py saved them over the 16 Oct bodies of the same URLs, and they parse to the same 300 distinct rows (event URL and start) as the eligibility parse. Removed: S1 1 by the Amendment 4 words; S2 1 canceled ('There will be no Paper Machete July 4th') and 1 untitled (16 Aug). All 183 nights are on a live grid, but the counts stay lower bounds: the grid drops past rows when a recurring event is replaced, and the further /calendar/ captures listed above and the 13 Apr captures of the Apr-Sep month views were not read (report.json, more_captures). One row with a Boise time zone in its Google Calendar link (3 May, 16:00) is written at the local time the grid shows. Test room, as `venues.csv` has it.
- Presenters, compiled (18 Oct 2026): the Chicago Jazz Composers Collective, written in two name forms ('Chicago Jazz Composers Collective' 5 rows, 'The Chicago Jazz Composers' 1), presents 6 of the 292 counted rows (S1 3, S2 3), so there is no house booker and the dry run lists none; the presenter check finds it an artist-run collective, not Live Nation or AEG. The 2 S2 rows that name a private-person bandleader as presenting his own band are not written. No LN/AEG presenter.
- Capacity: no figure published on the pages read.
- Requests during the eligibility check: greenmilljazz.com 21 (15 at 200, 6 at 404; robots.txt read included; 3 on 15 Oct UTC, 18 on 16 Oct UTC), web.archive.org 4 (2 CDX queries of /calendar*, the first a 503, 1 CDX query of /events/, 1 calendar copy; 16 Oct UTC). The second check on 18 Oct 2026 made no request and read only saved bodies (see requests.log).

### Martyrs' (`chi-martyrs`, Chicago)
- Source: The venue's own Drupal HTML calendar (https://martyrslive.com/calendar, the same page as /upcoming; one views-row per show with a schedule field (the node title: date, doors and show times, price), a buy-it-now field, a ticket field, a bands field and a description), plus the site's own Drupal feed https://martyrslive.com/rss.xml (the 10 newest nodes). The live calendar lists only coming shows (16 Oct 2026 to Jan 2027) and the venue host has no list of past shows (/calendar/2026-05 and /catalog are 404, sitemap.xml lists only the homepage, /node?page=N returns the homepage), so S1/S2 come from web.archive.org copies of the own /calendar. Every copy lists all announced future shows on one page, with no pager. Settled eligible from saved copies (see Coverage); the 15-17 Oct record had it undecided.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://martyrslive.com/calendar for ts 20260306024807, 20260310214037, 20260323172214, 20260418200929 and 20260714183313 (5 of the 6 captures in the CDX list; the sixth, 20260418162935, is the same day as a copy read and adds nothing; /upcoming has no capture); the web.archive.org CDX lists of martyrslive.com/calendar and martyrslive.com/upcoming (1 Mar to 15 Oct 2026, status 200), used only to see which copies exist; the live https://martyrslive.com/rss.xml (saved 16 Oct 2026 UTC); the live /calendar, homepage, /history, /contactus, /private-events and /tech for date, operator, room and terms checks.
- Dates checked: These are show dates. Live /calendar: 'Fri, Oct 16 Doors 7:30pm - Show 8:30pm' and 'Sun, Oct 18th - Doors 2PM - Show 3PM' (the weekly Sunday residency). rss.xml: 'Wed, Sept 23rd - Doors 7PM - Show 8PM' with 'Schedule: Sep 23 Time: 8pm'; its pubDate (05 Aug 2026) is the node's creation date, not the show date. Titles carry no year: it comes from the Google-Calendar link on each row (dates=YYYYDDMM, e.g. 20261610), whose day and month agree with the schedule text on every dated row. The Google-Calendar links write local times with a false 'Z' (8:30PM as T083000Z), so start is the schedule line's show time, else its doors time, local, no offset. The second check confirmed 8 rows drawn with seed 8 in the copy cited, e.g. 2026-04-09 20:00 (20260323 copy) and 2026-04-26 15:00 and 2026-07-10 20:00 (20260418 copy). No start lies between 00:01 and 04:59, so Amendment 3 moves no date.
- robots.txt (15 and 16 Oct 2026 UTC): allowed. https://martyrslive.com/robots.txt is the stock Drupal file: 'User-agent: *' with 'Crawl-delay: 10', disallowing only /includes/, /misc/, /modules/, /profiles/, /scripts/, /sites/, /themes/, /admin/, /comment/reply/, /contact/, /logout/, /node/add/, /search/, /user/register/, /user/password/ and /user/login/ (each also as /?q=...), and Drupal's own files (CHANGELOG.txt, cron.php, the INSTALL files, install.php, LICENSE.txt, MAINTAINERS.txt, update.php, UPGRADE.txt, xmlrpc.php); no Anthropic group. polite.py allowed /calendar, /upcoming, /rss.xml and every other page read (the 10 s delay was honoured). The archive copies were read on 17 Oct 2026 UTC by the prefetch through polite.py (30 s gap), after the stop of 16-17 Oct.
- Terms (15 and 16 Oct 2026 UTC; re-checked 17 Oct 2026 on the five saved copies): none-found. The site has no terms page and no terms text, and the homepage and footer link no terms or privacy page. Scans of the saved homepage, calendar and five archive copies for ©, copyright, all rights reserved, terms, privacy, reproduce, republish and personal use found only the Drupal search box tooltip 'Enter the terms you wish to search for.' and band prose. Self-hosted, so no platform terms apply. Checked: https://martyrslive.com/ (footer); https://martyrslive.com/calendar; https://martyrslive.com/contactus; https://martyrslive.com/private-events; https://martyrslive.com/tech; https://martyrslive.com/history; https://martyrslive.com/terms (404); https://martyrslive.com/terms-of-use (404); https://martyrslive.com/terms-of-service (404); https://martyrslive.com/terms-and-conditions (404); https://martyrslive.com/legal (404); https://martyrslive.com/privacy (404)
- Platform: self-hosted Drupal 6 (Zen theme) with Ubercart; most shows sell through the venue's own buy-it-now forms. Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Webflow, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer or Etix link on any saved body. A few rows sell elsewhere: tickets.venuepilot.com for two Serdar Presents nights (25 Mar, outside S1, and 23 Apr) and axs.com (skin=jam) for one S1 row (29 May); AXS is AEG's ticketing platform, which makes AEG neither owner nor booker (noted only). No bot check and no 403: every venue-host request was a 200 or a 404 (the six terms guesses, /calendar/2026-05 and /catalog), and all five archive copies are the real 'Calendar | Martyrs'' page (55-79 views-rows with Add to cart forms), with no captcha or challenge markup.
- Operator: a private owner (the founder, named on https://martyrslive.com/history, whose first-person account tells of signing the lease on the old post office building at 3855 North Lincoln in October 1994) and family; no company owner is identified, and no public source read names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake, booker or sale (searches of 2025-2026 news included). House booker: the venue's own booking office ('For promoters interested in bringing shows into Martyrs', contact Martyrs' booking office for dates, terms, and other details.', https://martyrslive.com/private-events; band booking on https://martyrslive.com/contactus goes to the venue's own staff, private persons named there). Outside promoters named on the 84 counted S1 and S2 rows: Serdar Presents 1 (23 Apr, the ticket field's own text, linking dromnyc.com), Jam Productions 1 (29 May, the ticket field's link text, linking jamusa.com) and KYMCO USA 1 (4 Sep, 'KYMCO USA presents' in the description), each 1 of 84. Jam Productions is an independent Chicago promoter; the second check found press describing it as a competitor of Live Nation and AEG, and no Live Nation or AEG stake. No company presents more than half of the rows, so the operator is the venue itself and Martyrs' is its own cluster. §1 passes. The second check notes that the Jam Productions credit comes only from a ticket link's text, not from a 'presents' line, so the literal-reading `presented_by` of that row may be read as empty; that is descriptive only.
- Rooms: one. The calendar and the event nodes have no room or stage field and no filter (the view fields are only schedule, buy-it-now, ticket URL, bands, description and share markup; the Google-Calendar location is empty), and /tech describes one stage on the ground floor. The settle wrote the rows as `martyrslive-com:house`; that is the whole venue, not a leftover pool room of a split building (Amendment 14's pool-room rule is for rows of a split venue), so it is the venue's one room and a test room.
- Coverage: S1 >=45, S2 >=39 for the one room (lower bounds; by month Apr 18, May 20, Jun 7; Jul 14, Aug 14, Sep 11), settled on 17-18 Oct 2026 from saved copies (no network) after web.archive.org stopped the 16-17 Oct check; the 15-17 Oct record had only S1 >=0, S2 >=6 from rss.xml. Union of the five /calendar copies and rss.xml by Drupal node id (one row with no node id keyed by night and bands), the latest copy listing a show winning; counted rows come from the 23 Mar copy (10), the 18 Apr copy (40), the 14 Jul copy (29) and rss.xml (5). 0 canceled (the only CANCELLED row, 31 Mar, is outside S1), 0 off-site, 0 untitled, 0 Amendment 4 word hits. One S2 row (13 Sep, 1pm-5pm, free, from rss.xml) is a celebration of life for a private person named in its title; it is withheld from `shows.csv` and from the count as a likely private memorial, and with it S2 would be 40. An independent second check with its own parser upheld both counts and corrected none; it matched every row of `shows.csv` both ways. The counts are lower bounds because shows announced between copies and played before the next one are not seen: 1-17 Apr rest on copies up to 23 Mar, 18 Apr to 13 Jul only on the 18 Apr copy (no capture exists from 19 Apr to 13 Jul, hence June's 7), and 14 Jul to 30 Sep on the 14 Jul copy plus rss.xml (no capture after 14 Jul; rss.xml added the 22 Aug, 16 Sep and 23 Sep shows). No show listed in an earlier copy for a later night is missing from a later copy. A show canceled after the last copy that lists it would not show; S2 would have to lose 20 rows to go under 20.
- Coverage, compiled (18 Oct 2026, Amendment 12): the compiled `shows.csv` (84 rows: 10 from the 23 Mar copy, 40 from the 18 Apr copy, 29 from the 14 Jul copy and 5 from rss.xml; saved bodies only, no request) gives S1 45, S2 39 for the one room, the settle's counts. 0 canceled, 0 off-site, 0 untitled, 0 removed by the Amendment 4 words. The 13 Sep memorial row stays withheld (S2 would be 40; report.json, withheld_rows). Every night is covered by a copy dated before it, with the lag windows given above, so the counts stay lower bounds. Test room, as `venues.csv` has it.
- Presenters, compiled (18 Oct 2026): Serdar Presents 1 (23 Apr), Jam Productions 1 (29 May, the ticket field's link text) and KYMCO USA 1 (4 Sep, kept from the 14 Jul copy's description; the row's other fields come from rss.xml), 3 of the 84 counted rows, so there is no house booker and the dry run lists none. The presenter check finds none of them Live Nation or AEG (Jam Productions is an independent Chicago promoter in which SaveLive took an equity stake in 2022). No LN/AEG presenter.
- Capacity: no figure published by the venue (/tech, /private-events and /contactus give none). A third-party listing's 320 is not used (Amendment 8).
- §5: the Chicago metro (Chicago-Naperville-Elgin, IL-IN MSA, OMB Bulletin 23-01) has eligible venues of other clusters: Reggies Chicago (test rooms Rock Club, Music Joint and Comedy Shack) and Old Town School of Folk Music (pool), both settled 17 Oct 2026, and Green Mill (test, one room), which `candidates.md` had "not used" on §5 alone and which was re-admitted on §5 on 18 Oct 2026 through Martyrs' and Reggies Chicago. So §5 passes. §6 and Amendment 11: Chicago is not Study 1's metro, and Martyrs' is not Hideout.
- Requests during the eligibility check: martyrslive.com 26 (requests.log: 4 on 15 Oct 2026 UTC, 19 on 16 Oct UTC, 22:33-22:37, and 3 in the second check the same night, 22:39-22:40; 2 of them robots.txt reads; 17 at 200, 9 at 404; the record's figure of 23 leaves out the second check's 3), web.archive.org 7 (17 Oct 2026 UTC, the prefetch: 2 CDX lists, for /calendar and /upcoming, and the 5 /calendar copies). The settle and its second check made no request.
- Note for compiling: node titles are date and price strings; the act names are in the bands field, which also holds non-act entries ('Tickets Available at the Door!', 'Click Here To Tip The Hoyle Brothers!', 'First Wednesday of the Month'), kept verbatim in `performers` and `title`. The headliner rule and the Amendment 4 word check must read the bands field, not the node title alone (the second check matched the words on the bands field and the schedule field).

### Old Town School of Folk Music (`chi-oldtown-*`, Chicago)
- Source: The venue's own site on a custom CMS: the HTML concert listing (/concerts/), whose rows give a date line '<weekday> · <date> · <time> · <room>' apart from the h3 title, and the events JSON array embedded in /calendar/ (fields URL, UTCstart, UTCend, eventName, location, status, type). Event pages are /concerts/YYYY/MM-DD-YYYY-<slug>. The live site lists only 1 Oct 2026 onward (the calendar JSON starts 2026-10-01; /concerts/2026/ and /sitemap.xml return 404; there is no past index), so S1/S2 come from web.archive.org copies of the same two own pages. The calendar's 'Workshop' rows are class pages (/classes/detail/?courseid=...), 134 over the two calendar copies; they are not shows and were not written. 'Field Trip' rows (school-day concerts with /concerts/2026/ pages) were kept.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.oldtownschool.org/concerts/ for ts 20260310145016, 20260320001233, 20260414112132, 20260427174232, 20260523232113, 20260613030232, 20260722191640, 20260920023130; https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.oldtownschool.org/calendar/ for ts 20260310032801, 20260411233658; CDX queries for oldtownschool.org/concerts/ and oldtownschool.org/calendar/ (with and without www). The CDX lists 14 captures of /concerts/ and 3 of /calendar/ (none after 20260411233658). Live https://www.oldtownschool.org/concerts/, /calendar/, /concerts/about/, /aboutus/ and one event page, for the date, operator, terms and capacity checks only. Round 2 (copies saved on 18 Oct 2026, 18:36-18:49 UTC): the /concerts/ copy 20260917011429, a prefix CDX query on oldtownschool.org/concerts/2026/ (23 rows) and its 23 event-page copies, https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.oldtownschool.org/concerts/2026/<MM-DD-YYYY-slug>/.
- Dates checked: These are show dates. Live event page /concerts/2026/10-16-2026-fugu-dugu reads 'Friday, October 16, 2026 · 7:00 PM CDT', room Szold Hall, with a ticket price and BUY button; the live listing row 'Saturday · October 17 2026 · 8:00 PM CDT · Maurer Hall' links to /concerts/2026/10-17-2026-.... Copy 20260920023130: 'Saturday · September 19 2026 · 8:00 PM CDT · Szold Hall' over the h3 title, written 2026-09-19T20:00:00-05:00 in Szold Hall. Calendar UTCstart values were converted to America/Chicago first (Amendment 3). The independent check's sample of 8 rows matched date, time, title and room in the named copy (one title lost a double space). No start falls between 00:01 and 04:59.
- robots.txt (16 Oct 2026): allowed. https://www.oldtownschool.org/robots.txt: 'User-agent: *' disallows /cart/, /concerts/preview/, /contact/, /events/, /fieldtrips/, /images/, /workshops/ and other paths not read, and disallows none of /concerts/, /concerts/2026/..., /calendar/ and /aboutus/; the only other group is facebookexternalhit; no Anthropic group. polite.py allowed every URL read; web.archive.org allowed. oldtownschool.org redirects to www.oldtownschool.org.
- Terms (16 Oct 2026): copyright-only. The footer of every page says only '© 2017 Old Town School of Folk Music' (a bare notice, which Amendment 14 says is not terms) and links no terms page. The privacy policy and /aboutus/policy/ have no use-licence, copying, reproduction, personal-use or scraping clause; the policy's only 'prohibited' line is about teaching non-students on the premises. The ticket policy's 'It is unlawful to reproduce this ticket in any form' covers tickets and recording, not site content. The 10 copies carry the same bare footer and no terms text. Checked: https://www.oldtownschool.org/ (footer); https://www.oldtownschool.org/concerts/about/; https://www.oldtownschool.org/aboutus/privacy/; https://www.oldtownschool.org/aboutus/policy/; https://www.oldtownschool.org/terms/ (404); https://www.oldtownschool.org/terms-of-use/ (404); https://www.oldtownschool.org/terms-and-conditions/ (404); https://www.oldtownschool.org/legal/ (404)
- Platform: Custom in-house CMS; no site-builder generator tag, no Squarespace, Wix, Webflow, Weebly, Fatsoma or Music Glue marker, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS' or Etix footer in the live pages or any copy. The only 'cloudflare' string is a stylesheet loaded from cdnjs.cloudflare.com, not a challenge. Every live page answered 200 or a plain 404, and the 10 copies are real listing pages with no captcha, challenge or 403 text. Tickets are sold on the school's own event pages and refunds go to 'your Old Town School account'; no Ticketmaster, AXS or Live Nation link.
- Operator (§1): Old Town School of Folk Music, a 501(c)(3) not-for-profit community arts school founded in 1957 (https://www.oldtownschool.org/aboutus/), owns and runs the Lincoln Square campus (4544 and 4545 N Lincoln Ave) and the Armitage Ave campus. The venue names its own booking office as the booker (https://www.oldtownschool.org/concerts/about/, Booking Policy: 'If you would like to request that we book an artist, please email your suggestion to bookings@oldtownschool.org'), so booking is in house and the owner is the operator. Outside presenters: only two counted S1 titles name a presenter (1 Apr Szold Hall, 29 Apr Maurer Hall), and in both the presenter is the performing ensemble itself; one S2 row (22 Sep) has a subtitle in which the act presents its own programme. No company presents any counted row, so none is a house booker (Amendment 14). Web searches (17 Oct 2026) and the saved bodies name no Live Nation, AEG, Rockhouse or Etix owner, stake, booker or ticketer.
- §5: the Chicago metro (Chicago-Naperville-Elgin, IL-IN MSA, OMB Bulletin 23-01) has eligible venues of other clusters: Martyrs' (test, one room) and Reggies Chicago (test rooms Rock Club, Music Joint and Comedy Shack), settled 17 Oct 2026, and Green Mill (test, one room), re-admitted on §5 on 18 Oct 2026. So §5 passes. §6 and Amendment 11: Chicago is not Study 1's Minneapolis-St Paul metro, and Old Town School of Folk Music is neither Hideout nor Black Cat.
- Rooms: the room in the listing's date line and the calendar's 'location' field, never the title (Amendment 14, rooms). Maurer Hall (4544 N Lincoln Ave, west building) and Szold Hall (4545 N Lincoln Ave, east building, across the street) are counted as one building, the Lincoln Square campus. Rows that give only '4544 N Lincoln Ave -' (6 field trips) or no room (Fretboard Summit, 20 Aug) go to the house room `chi-oldtown-house`. Armitage Hall (909 W Armitage Ave, 'Armitage Concert Hall' in the calendar) is a separate building, counted on its own: 0 / 0 (its only S1 rows are three 'Teen Open Mic' nights, removed by word; no S2 row), so it is not eligible and not listed, and its rows are `off-site` for this venue. One row moved rooms between copies (10 Apr: Maurer in the March listing copies, Szold in the 11 Apr calendar copy, and Maurer Hall again on its event-page copy of 8 Jun); the latest copy, Maurer Hall, is used. Capacity: Maurer Hall 430 and Szold Hall 170, the figures https://www.oldtownschool.org/concerts/about/ publishes ('Maximum capacity of Maurer Hall is 430. Maximum capacity of Szold Hall is 170.'; the same page also says 150 for Szold Hall, and the largest figure applies, Amendment 8).
- Coverage: S1 >=55, S2 >=22 for the building (lower bounds; round 2, 18 Oct 2026). By room: Maurer Hall 33 / 18, Szold Hall 18 / 3, house 4 / 1; Armitage Hall (other building) 0 / 0. No room meets 20 / 20 by itself, and the house room holds the rows with no room, so every room is pool and the venue is listed as pool (Amendment 14, pool-only venues). An independent recount of the same 34 bodies gives exactly these counts. Round 2 read 34 distinct copies: the 2 /calendar/ and 8 /concerts/ copies of round 1, the /concerts/ copy 20260917011429 and 23 event-page copies. Changes from round 1 (Maurer Hall 32 / 17, building 55 / 21): the 17 Sep copy adds one S2 show in Maurer Hall (16 Sep, a World Music Wednesday night); the event-page copy of 8 Jun moves the 10 Apr show from Szold Hall to Maurer Hall (the latest copy wins); and the early and late sets of four shows (11 Apr, 23 Apr, 24 Apr and 9 May) are separate rows, since the same copy lists both starts. The 23 event pages add no other in-period show; they confirm room and time.
