Canceled S1: 6 (calendar status 'Cancelled' on one Maurer show, 19 Jun, and two field trips, 1 May; three Maurer nights postponed and seen only across copies, 3 and 4 Apr moved to 29-30 May and 13 Jun moved to 8 Aug, written canceled on the old dates, the new dates counting). Off-site S1: 2 (10 Apr at the Athenaeum; 18 Apr, day 2 of a symposium, at Arts and Public Life, Garfield Park). Counted S1 rows include daytime field trips for school groups; without them Maurer Hall S1 is 31. S2: 22 listed and not canceled, none removed. The S2 margin is 2: it includes 'Fretboard Summit 2026 - 3-day All Access' (20 Aug, house room), and it rests on 4544 and 4545 N Lincoln counting as one building (as two, neither meets S2: 4544 N Lincoln 18, or 19 with the Fretboard Summit row, which names no room, and 4545 N Lincoln 3). A show announced after one copy and played before the next is not seen; the lag windows are 28 Apr - 23 May and 24 May - 12 Jun in S1, 13 Jun - 22 Jul and 23 Jul - 16 Sep in S2 (the 22 Jul copy lists from 25 Jul, the 17 Sep copy from 16 Sep, and no saved capture of /concerts/ or /calendar/ falls between them). The spring World Music Wednesday run, almost all in Maurer Hall, ends on 24 Jun and the fall run is first seen from 16 Sep, so July and August look like a break, but late-announced Maurer shows between 23 Jul and 15 Sep would not be seen. Maurer Hall needs 2 more S2 rows to be a test room. The saved CDX files hold no other capture in the S2 lag windows; the plan task `settle:oldtownschool-org-gap` (a prefix CDX query on oldtownschool.org/concerts/ for 14 Jun - 16 Sep, up to 30 pages) was still open in the prefetch plan at 22:32 server time on 18 Oct: its one try (22:20:23) got no answer and queued nothing. If Maurer Hall became a test room, Chicago would hold 4 test clusters, the 20th test cluster would be in Cleveland and ALICE would leave the cap. Settled on 17 Oct and again on 18 Oct 2026 from saved copies only, after web.archive.org stopped the 16-17 Oct check. More rows can only raise these counts. The round-2 `shows.csv` (settle) writes the two off-site rows in the house room with drop_reason `off-site`, and the 3 Armitage Hall rows with the room Armitage Hall and no drop_reason; when compiling, those three go to a building room with drop_reason `off-site`.
- Coverage, compiled (18 Oct 2026, Amendment 12): the compiled `shows.csv` (90 rows from the 34 saved copies; no request) gives Maurer Hall 33 / 18, Szold Hall 20 / 3 and the house room 4 / 1; building 57 / 22. Szold Hall S1 is 2 above the round-2 count: The Working Songwriter Forum, one row (5 Jun) in the listing and calendar copies, is written as three date-only Szold Hall rows (5, 6 and 7 Jun), as its own event-page copy 20260613025247, the latest capture, gives them; with one row Szold Hall S1 would be 18. Removed: S1 6 canceled (Maurer Hall 4, house room 2) and 5 off-site (all in the house room: 10 Apr, 18 Apr and the 3 Armitage Hall 'Teen Open Mic' rows); S2 none. No night is a gap (report.json lists the lag windows). Every room stays pool, as `venues.csv` has it; Maurer Hall still needs 2 more S2 rows to be a test room.
- Presenters, compiled (18 Oct 2026): 2 of the 79 counted rows name a presenter, and in both it is the performing ensemble: a sextet billed under its leader's name (Maurer Hall, 29 Apr) and Apollo's Fire, written with its director's name as the title gives it (Szold Hall, 1 Apr); both are written in `presented_by` as the titles give them. 2 rows whose act, billed under its members' personal names, presents its own programme (Szold Hall 30 May, Maurer Hall 22 Sep) are not written. No house booker (the dry run lists none) and no LN/AEG presenter.
- Requests during the eligibility check: www.oldtownschool.org 26 (4 on 15 Oct, 18 on 16 Oct by the first check, 4 by the second check), oldtownschool.org 2 (15 Oct), web.archive.org 49 naming oldtownschool.org (24 on 17 Oct: 4 CDX queries and 20 copy reads, each of the 10 copies read twice because a task with and one without www both queued it; 25 on 18 Oct, 18:36-18:49 UTC, all answered 200: the 20260917011429 copy, one prefix CDX query and 23 event-page copies), plus 1 refusal logged without sending on 16 Oct while the archive was stopped (see requests.log).

### Reggies Chicago (`chi-reggies-*`, Chicago)
- Source: The venue's own self-hosted WordPress site. Each show is a WordPress post with a dated <article> (<time datetime=YYYY-MM-DD>, doors and show times, a venue-* taxonomy class that names the room), listed on the homepage and on the room filters /venue/<room>/, with one page per show at /show/<slug>/ (indexed by the AIOSEO show sitemaps). The live listings, the venue and date archives and the RSS feeds show coming shows only, and the calendar feed (admin-ajax.php, action myajax-submit) is POST-only and answers null to GET, so S1/S2 come from web.archive.org copies of the venue's own homepage and room filters, plus six own show pages read live.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.reggieslive.com/ for ts 20260318123652, 20260414180711, 20260501173729, 20260515224501, 20260527052331, 20260609051825, 20260712180426, 20260811020630, 20260822035647, 20260930211424, and of https://reggieslive.com/ for ts 20260301225533, 20260315204410 (12 homepage copies, picked from 33 CDX rows); https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.reggieslive.com/venue/rock-club/ for ts 20260301031835, 20260415153056, 20260515233943 (3 of 5 CDX rows); https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.reggieslive.com/venue/music-joint/ for ts 20260308110022, 20260406172139, 20260608121449, 20260712180958, and of https://reggieslive.com/venue/music-joint/ for ts 20260417005408 (5 of 7 CDX rows); three CDX queries (statuscode 200, 1 Mar to 15 Oct 2026). Live (16 Oct 2026 UTC): https://www.reggieslive.com/show/<slug>/ for parker-barrow-2, oranssi-pazuzu-2, leftover-crack-3, vader-6, six-feet-under-2 and flamenco-night-at-reggies-on-the-beach; the homepage, show-sitemap.xml, page-sitemap.xml and the about (with its rock-club and music-joint pages), booking, FAQ, privacy and plan-your-event pages; and, to look for past listings, sitemap.xml, the April date archive, three RSS feeds, the REST types list, the calendar page, its script and two GETs of its feed (none lists past shows). All 20 copies answered 200 and are real listings (42 to 95 show articles each, gzip bodies).
- Dates checked: The six live show pages give the date the flyer gives: Parker Barrow 2026-06-19 (music-joint, 9:00 pm show), Oranssi Pazuzu 2026-05-17 (rock-club, 8:30 pm show), Leftover Crack 2026-06-17 (rock-club), Vader 2026-09-27 (rock-club, 7:00 pm show), Six Feet Under 2026-08-11 (rock-club), the flamenco night at Reggies on the Beach 2026-08-23 (venue-beach, another building). In the copies each article's date is its <time datetime> value; the start is the 'Show' time, else the doors time, else the only time. 'MIDNIGHT' is read as 00:00 and the listed date is kept (Amendment 3); those rows are Open Mic Comedy nights, which the Amendment 4 words drop anyway. No start falls between 00:01 and 04:59. The skeptic found 8 random rows of `shows.csv` (seed 7) in the bodies their source_url names, with the same date, title and venue-* class. These are show dates, read at the America/Chicago local date.
- robots.txt (16 Oct 2026 UTC): allowed. https://www.reggieslive.com/robots.txt: one block disallows only /wp-content/uploads/wpo-plugins-tables-list.json, and the Yoast block says 'Disallow:' (empty); no Anthropic group. polite.py allowed every URL read. reggieslive.com redirects to www.reggieslive.com. web.archive.org served every copy and CDX query (17 Oct 2026 UTC). No 403 and no bot check on any request, and no copy holds a captcha, challenge, 'just a moment' or 'access denied' text.
- Terms (16 Oct 2026 UTC): none-found. No terms page exists (page-sitemap.xml lists none) and the footer has no terms link. The footer reads only '© 2026 Reggies Chicago' plus site credits (private persons, not written here), a bare notice (Amendment 14). The privacy notice of Reggie's Bar & Grill, LLC (last updated Aug 2, 2024) has no use-licence, copying or personal-viewing clause; its only uses of 'copy' are about data-subject rights. The FAQ, About, Booking and Plan-your-event pages have no reuse restriction. The 20 archive copies carry the same footer and no terms or 'powered by' text. Checked: https://www.reggieslive.com/ (footer); https://www.reggieslive.com/page-sitemap.xml; https://www.reggieslive.com/contact/privacy-policy/; https://www.reggieslive.com/faq/; https://www.reggieslive.com/about/; https://www.reggieslive.com/contact/booking/; https://www.reggieslive.com/menu/plan-your-event/
- Platform: Self-hosted WordPress with a custom theme 'reggies' (AIOSEO 4.6.0 and Yoast, W3 Total Cache). Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Weebly, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer or RHP plugin in the live pages or in any archive copy, so no platform terms apply. Ticket links go to TicketWeb, a Ticketmaster/Live Nation ticket vendor, which sells tickets only and does not host the site.
- Operator: Reggie's Bar & Grill, LLC, named as website owner in the privacy notice ('This privacy notice for Reggie's Bar & Grill, LLC ...'; 'Cookies set by the website owner (in this case, Reggie's Bar & Grill, LLC)', https://www.reggieslive.com/contact/privacy-policy/); the person behind it is a private owner (named in https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reggies, from a 2010 source). Open since 8 Sep 2007 (https://www.reggieslive.com/about/). House booking: 'BOOK YOUR BAND AT REGGIES PLAY THE MUSIC JOINT / ROCK CLUB We book a wide variety of musical acts and special events.', with three staff addresses @reggieslive.com (https://www.reggieslive.com/contact/booking/). Outside presenters among counted S1 + S2 rows (the presented-by line, when it says 'present(s)'): Rock Club (87 rows) Empire Productions 28 (32%), Riot Fest 15, Spark'd 7, Live Nation 2 and C3 1 (on a 'Reggies & C3 presents' row; C3 is Live Nation-linked, so 3 of 87 in all), every other company 3 or fewer; Music Joint (193 rows) Empire Productions 30, Magicswirl 4 (spelled two ways), every other company 2 or fewer; Banannas Comedy Shack (68 rows) Nolen Entertainment 3, every other company 1; Trainwreck Rooftop Deck (11 rows) none. 3 counted Rock Club rows name a private person as presenter (not written). No company presents more than half of any room's counted shows, so none is a house booker and none joins a cluster (Amendment 14); Empire Productions is not Live Nation or AEG. No public source found names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake or house booker for Reggies or Empire Productions.
- Rooms: the venue-* taxonomy class of each article, separate from the title, which the /venue/<room>/ filters also give (Amendment 14): Rock Club, Music Joint, Comedy Shack (Banannas Comedy Shack; TicketWeb calls it 'Reggies Banannas Shack'), Roof Deck (Trainwreck Rooftop Deck: the About page's 'REGGIES TRAINWRECK ROOFTOP DECK' without the building name) and Beach. Beach is Reggies on the Beach, held at the 63rd St. Beach ('Show will be at the 63rd St. Beach location'), another building, so its rows are `off-site`. The class always agrees with the /venue/<room>/ side icon where one is present; no article has two rooms and no in-period article lacks one, so there is no house room. A few titles are listed in more than one room on the same night, one article per room (Legions of Metal Fest 2026 on 1 and 2 May in the Rock Club and the Music Joint; School Of Rock Chicago on 27 Jun in three rooms and on 30 Aug in two; Pancakes and Booze Art Show on 18 Jul in two); each counts once in each room it names.
- Coverage: settled on 17 Oct 2026 UTC from the copies read that day (the settle made no request of its own); the 16 Oct eligibility check found web.archive.org stopped and had counted only 3 S1 and 2 S2 dated rows. Lower bounds, union of the 20 archive copies and the 6 live show pages by show slug, the latest copy's fields winning: Rock Club S1 >=51, S2 >=36; Music Joint >=105 / >=88; Banannas Comedy Shack >=42 / >=26; Trainwreck Rooftop Deck >=6 / >=5; building >=204 / >=155. An independent skeptic recounted every figure with its own parser and union (462 rows in the periods, the same rows as `shows.csv`) and upheld them. Removed by the Amendment 4 words in the post title: S1 36, S2 33 (Roof Deck 'bingo' 26, Music Joint 'open mic' 21, Comedy Shack 'open mic' 19 and 'comedy' 3). Off-site: S1 1, S2 33 (Beach). Canceled: 0. Gaps: the homepage copies cover 1 Apr to 30 Sep except 9 Aug (the last, possibly partial, day of the 12 Jul copy) and 22 Sep (partial) to 29 Sep; no homepage capture exists between 20260822035647 and 20260930211424, none of /venue/rock-club/ after 20260515233943 and none of /venue/music-joint/ between 20260712180958 and 20261012075113. For the Rock Club and the Music Joint those days come only from older room-filter copies (the 15 May Rock Club copy lists shows to 23 Oct, the 12 Jul Music Joint copy to 20 Oct), which miss shows announced later (Vader, 27 Sep, comes only from its live page); the Comedy Shack and the Roof Deck have no copy for 22-29 Sep, and each copy's first day can miss shows already over. 10 shows listed in one copy are missing from a later copy that covers their date, with no cancel mark (Rock Club 3, Comedy Shack 2, Music Joint 3, Roof Deck 1, Beach 1); they are counted. Without them: Rock Club 49 / 35, Music Joint 105 / 85, Comedy Shack 40 / 26, Roof Deck 6 / 5. The settle report gave Music Joint S2 86 here; the skeptic corrected it to 85 (three vanished Music Joint S2 rows, none word-dropped), and its figure is used. Two Rock Club S2 rows are one act on one night (The Impossibles, 22 Aug, 16:30 and 21:00), which the run counts once (Amendment 12, `duplicate`), so the Rock Club's S2 is 35 act-nights, 34 without the vanished row. Rock Club, Music Joint and Banannas Comedy Shack each meet coverage by themselves, so they are three test rooms of one cluster (`chi-reggies`); the Roof Deck is pool and cannot reach 20 from any capture. `shows.csv` confirms or corrects the roles (Amendment 12). Title choice: the title is the post title; the h2 billing line instead gives Rock Club 51 / 36, Music Joint 104 / 89 and Comedy Shack 54 / 34 (skeptic), so no room's role changes. Counted rows include non-music nights that carry none of the Amendment 4 words (White Sox and Crosstown parties, Free Sox Sundays, a brewery bus shuttle, Tipsy Tuesday, South Loop Stand Up), and the Comedy Shack is mostly stand-up; it is a test room because most stand-up titles lack the words, the rule applied as written (Amendment 4 is a word rule; a room's programme is not an eligibility test).
- Coverage, compiled (18 Oct 2026, Amendment 12): the compiled `shows.csv` (470 rows; shows keyed by WordPress post id, not slug) gives Rock Club 51 / 38, Music Joint 105 / 87, Banannas Comedy Shack 42 / 27 and Trainwreck Rooftop Deck 6 / 5; building 204 / 157. Written rows: Rock Club 51 / 38, Music Joint 117 / 99, Comedy Shack 55 / 37 and Roof Deck 19 / 54, the Roof Deck's including the 37 Beach rows (S1 1, S2 36), written there as `off-site`. Removed by the Amendment 4 words: S1 37, S2 35 (Music Joint 12 / 12, Comedy Shack 13 / 10, Roof Deck 12 / 13); canceled 0. Changes from the settle: keying by post id keeps both posts of four reused slugs of a weekly open-mic series, makes two retitled posts one row each (6 Apr, 8 Sep) and finds two reused Beach posts (+3 off-site rows), so Music Joint S2 is 87, not 88; and the compile made 4 live requests to www.reggieslive.com through polite.py (18 Oct, 19:02 UTC; web.archive.org 0) for the own pages of in-period shows that the copies name only in their 'Hot List' sidebar: charming-disaster-3, greenleaf and yhwh-nailgun answered 200 and gave 3 rows, drag-talk (30 Sep) answered 404 and gave none. Without those 3 rows Rock Club S2 is 36 and Comedy Shack S2 26. 6 shows that drop out of a later copy without a cancel mark are kept with canceled 0 (Rock Club 3, Music Joint 2, Comedy Shack 1); without them Rock Club is 49 / 37, Music Joint 105 / 85 and Comedy Shack 41 / 27. Gaps: the Comedy Shack and the Roof Deck have no copy for 9 Aug or 22-29 Sep, and no CDX capture covers those nights; Rock Club and Music Joint nights in those stretches come only from older room-filter copies. No role changes: Rock Club, Music Joint and Comedy Shack are test rooms and the Roof Deck is pool, as `venues.csv` has them.
- Presenters, compiled (18 Oct 2026; names as the rows write them, in lower case): Rock Club, 55 of 89 counted rows name a presenter: empire productions 28 and empire production 1, riot fest 16, spark'd 7, civl 3, kickstand productions 2, chicagoland hardcore 2, live nation 2, and 1 each for c3 (with reggies), third impact, midwest ska fest, artfinix studio, legends in session, urban grind tv, bigger than the city, societas music group, reggies and fortune ferret productions; Music Joint, 40 of 192: empire productions 30, magicswirl 2 and magickswirl 2, music made in america 2, and 1 each for capicola booking, chicago rock ensamble tributos, spark'd, reggies, viva la muerte and nolen entertainment; Banannas Comedy Shack, 7 of 69: nolen entertainment 3 and 1 each for dcase, magickswirl, red thread and sos militia; Trainwreck Rooftop Deck, 0 of 11. 3 Rock Club rows (S1 2, S2 1) name only a private person as presenter, who is not written, and 3 rows with an Empire Productions show tag but no 'presents' line (Music Joint 27 Jun and 4 Sep, Rock Club 5 Apr) are not credited. The largest share, Empire Productions on 29 of 89 Rock Club rows in its two spellings, is under half, so no room has a house booker and the dry run lists none; the operator stays Reggie's Bar & Grill, LLC. LN/AEG by the presenter check (`ln-aeg.csv`): live nation, live-nation, on 2 counted Rock Club rows (4 and 18 Jun, 'live nation presents'), and c3, live-nation (C3 Presents, L.L.C. is on Live Nation's FY2025 Exhibit 21.1), on 1 (30 Jul, 'reggies & c3 presents', a Lollapalooza aftershow): 3 of 89 Rock Club rows. Under the literal reading of Amendment 1 the dry run drops all four Reggies rooms on §1. Of the other names, 15 were not identified by the check and the rest are not Live Nation or AEG (riot fest an independent Chicago festival, civl the Chicago Independent Venue League, dcase the City of Chicago's Department of Cultural Affairs and Special Events, reggies the venue itself).
- §5: Chicago has eligible venues of other clusters, settled the same way: Martyrs' (test) and Old Town School of Folk Music (pool). §6: Chicago is not Minneapolis-St Paul, and Amendment 11 excludes Hideout, not Reggies.
- Capacity: Rock Club 350 ('Rock Club (350 person standing room music venue with stage and full bar on 1st floor)'), Music Joint 110 ('110 person main bar and grill with stage on the 1st floor'), Banannas Comedy Shack 110 ('110 person private event space with full bar, bathrooms and sound on 2nd floor'), Trainwreck Rooftop Deck 100 ('Rooftop (100 person, 3rd floor rooftop bar ...)'), all from https://www.reggieslive.com/menu/plan-your-event/; the About page calls the roof deck a '99 person rooftop bar', and the larger figure is used (Amendment 8).
- Note for compiling: the settle `shows.csv` writes venue_id `reggieslive-com:<room>`; map Rock Club to `chi-reggies-rockclub`, Music Joint to `chi-reggies-musicjoint`, Comedy Shack to `chi-reggies-comedyshack`, Roof Deck to `chi-reggies-roofdeck`, and keep the Beach rows dropped as `off-site`. Titles are the post titles; there is no structured performer field, so Amendment 2 does not apply. The 10 vanished rows are in `shows.csv` with canceled 0; Horseshoe Tavern and The Pearl leave such rows out when compiling (below), and no room's role changes either way. The 16 Oct record gives the cluster as `reggies-bar-and-grill-llc`; the merged rows use `chi-reggies`, one building either way.
- Requests during the eligibility check: www.reggieslive.com 28 (2 of them robots.txt reads), reggieslive.com 3 (2 answered with a redirect to www.reggieslive.com), all on 16 Oct 2026 23:20-23:25 UTC; web.archive.org 0 (the archive was stopped). Afterwards, for the settle: web.archive.org 23 on 17 Oct 2026 19:47-19:58 UTC (3 CDX queries and the 20 copies, all 200; see requests.log). The settle and its skeptic made no request.

### Beachland Ballroom & Tavern (`cle-beachland-*`, Cleveland)
- Source: The venue's own Webflow show list at https://www.beachlandballroom.com/shows. Every listing card carries a weekday/month/day sticker, an optional presenter line, the title, a headliner field, a support-artist field, a room field of its own (Beachland Ballroom, Beachland Tavern, This Way Out, or another building), Doors/Show times and a slug at /shows/<slug>; a calendar block on the same page gives each slug its full date with the year. The live list holds only coming shows (14 Oct 2026 onward), so S1/S2 come from the three web.archive.org copies of that list in the period and from the web.archive.org CDX index of the /shows/<slug> pages. Re-admitted on §5 on 18 Oct 2026, after a second check under the 16 Oct readings, because Music Box Supper Club (Cleveland, another cluster) was settled eligible on 17 Oct; `candidates.md` had it "not used" on §5 alone.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.beachlandballroom.com/shows for ts 20260302085057, 20260510085051 and 20260904032750 (the only three /shows captures from 15 Feb to 14 Oct 2026, per the CDX query collapsed by day; the 2 Mar and 4 Sep copies were saved gzip-encoded and were read decompressed, 361,142 and 379,928 bytes; the 10 May copy is 385,222 bytes); the CDX list of beachlandballroom.com/shows/ by prefix (from 1 Jan to 14 Oct 2026, collapsed by URL, 444 rows: 439 at 200, 4 at 404, 1 at 301); live https://www.beachlandballroom.com/shows and https://www.beachlandballroom.com/shows/second-nature-31-oct for date and room checks only, and the about, booking, privacy and terms paths below.
- Dates checked: These are show dates. Live list row Second Nature, Sat Oct 31, Beachland Tavern, Doors 7:00 pm / Show 8:00 pm = event page 'Beachland Tavern', 'October 31, 2026', 'Doors: 7:00 pm / Show: 8:00 pm'. In every S1/S2 card of the three copies the weekday/month/day sticker agrees with the calendar block's full date. Times are local wall-clock values; across the S1/S2 cards the latest show time is 8:30 pm and the earliest 10:00 am, so no start falls between 00:01 and 04:59 and Amendment 3 moves no date. The slug's date is not the card's date for 11 of the 285 slugs listed in the three copies (13 if the 5 slugs that end in a short code after the date are read through that code; some are moved shows: one card reads 'Rescheduled from DECEMBER 2, 2025'), so the card's date is used, at the latest copy that lists it. Three slugs change date between copies: one Ballroom row moves within S1 (1 Apr to 2 Jun), one moves from 4 Mar to 16 Oct, and one Ballroom row moves out of S1 (12 Jun to 22 Oct 2026) and is not counted.
- robots.txt (14 Oct 2026 UTC): allowed. https://www.beachlandballroom.com/robots.txt returned 200 with an empty body (0 bytes), which allows every path; no Anthropic group. polite.py allowed every URL read; web.archive.org's robots.txt allows the snapshot and CDX paths used. No bot check and no 403 from the venue host: every venue-host request was a 200 (7) or a 404 (6). The only 'captcha' is the reCAPTCHA of the newsletter form, and the only 'cloudflare' strings are cdnjs.cloudflare.com calendar scripts and styles.
- Terms (14 Oct 2026 UTC; re-checked 18 Oct 2026 from the saved bodies): none-found. The footer links only Privacy in the three copies, and Privacy and Accessibility on the live pages, and reads '© , Beachland Ballroom'; the privacy page ends '© 2025 The Beachland Ballroom. All rights reserved.', a bare notice (Amendment 14). The privacy page covers only visitors' own data; it mentions 'our website terms', but no such page exists. A scan of all 12 saved venue-host bodies and the three listing copies found no text that forbids copying, reproducing, republishing or automated reading, or limits use to personal viewing. Checked: https://www.beachlandballroom.com/ (footer); https://www.beachlandballroom.com/privacy; https://www.beachlandballroom.com/about; https://www.beachlandballroom.com/booking; https://www.beachlandballroom.com/terms (404); https://www.beachlandballroom.com/terms-of-use (404); https://www.beachlandballroom.com/terms-of-service (404); https://www.beachlandballroom.com/terms-and-conditions (404); https://www.beachlandballroom.com/legal (404); https://www.beachlandballroom.com/sitemap.xml (404); https://www.opendate.io/terms (ticket platform; see Platform). The /accessibility page was not read.
- Platform: Webflow (data-wf-site and data-wf-page on every page, assets on cdn.prod.website-files.com; 'Last Published' stamps of 27 Feb, 8 May and 3 Sep 2026 in the three copies), which passes as for Horseshoe Tavern and Off Broadway; the footer reads 'Powered by Opendate', and tickets are sold through Opendate. Opendate's terms bind organizers, not the people who view an organizer's listings, as read for Off Broadway, so they do not decide the venue. Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Weebly, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer: 'rockhouse' and 'etix' occur in none of the 12 saved venue-host bodies or the three copies. Two cards of the 4 Sep copy and the live list, both at House of Blues on 31 Oct and 7 Nov 2026 (outside S1 and S2), link to Ticketmaster; the venue's own ticketing is Opendate. Some other cards link other ticket sellers (calendar block 'url' field): TicketWeb, a Ticketmaster brand, on 4 counted rows, all presented by Grog Shop (Ballroom 2 Jun (first listed for 1 Apr), 28 Jul and 17 Sep; Tavern 27 Sep 2026); AXS, AEG's ticketing, on off-site cards (Cleveland Agora 23 Jul 2026; Globe Iron 28 Oct 2026 and 13 Feb 2027) and, in the live list, on one Ballroom card of 12 Feb 2027, outside S1 and S2, so on no counted row; Dice on BravoArtist nights; Eventbrite on two cards outside S1 and S2. A ticket seller used for an outside presenter's nights is neither the owner nor the house booker, so these links do not decide §1. All three web.archive.org copies are the real page ('Shows | Beachland Ballroom', 104, 113 and 111 cards), not a bot-check or hosting-check page, so every copy counts.
- Operator: private co-owners, who founded the venue in 2000 (named on https://www.beachlandballroom.com/about, which says 'Beachland has been proudly independently owned and operated since 2000'). No company owner is identified. Booking is in-house: the booking page takes artists' submissions into the venue's own system ('you are in our system and on our radar') and names no booking company. No public source read names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake or house booker; the 15 Oct check found no news of a sale, and a 2025 press feature (freshwatercleveland.com) that names the same co-owners at the 25th anniversary was seen only as a search snippet (requests.log has only that host's robots.txt). Outside presenters on counted rows, from the presenter line: BravoArtist on 3 of 75 Ballroom rows and 4 of 99 Tavern rows, Grog Shop (another Cleveland club) on 4 Ballroom rows (one jointly with Beachland) and 1 Tavern row; every other presenter named has one row. No company presents more than half of either room's rows, so there is no outside house booker (Amendment 14), and the venue is its own cluster. `venues.csv` gives the venue's own name, Beachland Ballroom & Tavern, as the operator of its rooms, since its owners are private persons and no company is named. Live Nation is named once, 'Beachland & Live Nation Presents', and House of Blues Cleveland once, 'Beachland & House of Blues Cleveland', on the two House of Blues cards of Oct and Nov 2026: off-site, outside S1 and S2, and neither an owner nor a house booker, so §1 passes.
- Rooms: split. The room is a field of its own outside the title, so Beachland Ballroom and Beachland Tavern are rooms (the about page: 'two stages under one roof – the intimate Tavern stage (148 capacity) and the larger Ballroom stage (500 capacity)'). This Way Out, the vintage shop in the building's basement (about page), is also given in the room field (4 S2 cards, titled 'This Way Out', 12:00 pm), so it is a room; it fails coverage and is a pool room. CDX-only slugs carry no room and go to the house room, named after the building, which stays a pool room. Rows at Treelawn Social Club, Cleveland Agora, Treelawn Music Hall, Rock & Roll Hall of Fame, House of Blues and Globe Iron are other buildings (off-site).
- Coverage: Beachland Ballroom S1 >=43, S2 >=32; Beachland Tavern S1 >=64, S2 >=35; This Way Out S1 0, S2 4; house room (CDX-only slugs) S1 23, S2 13; building S1 >=130, S2 >=84. Listing rows are the union by slug of the three copies (182 S1/S2 cards), dated at the latest copy that lists them. Taken out: off-site (Amendment 1), S1 2 (Treelawn Social Club) and S2 1 (Cleveland Agora); Amendment 4 words, S2 1 (Ballroom, 'XCSB Flea Market', 'market'); one Ballroom row moved from 12 Jun to 22 Oct 2026. No card is marked canceled or postponed (the only badge is 'Sold out') and no title is empty. One Tavern S2 row (16 Sep) is listed in the 2 Mar and 10 May copies and gone from the 4 Sep copy without a mark; it is counted, and Tavern S2 is >=34 without it. The CDX-only rows are dated by the slug's day and month in 2026, 222 slugs in all, 36 of them in S1/S2, none with an Amendment 4 word; 6 CDX-only slugs end in a short code after the date and were left undated, and read through that code one more (a 'this-way-out' shop day, 12 Apr) falls in S1, so the house room's S1 is 23 or 24; since 11 listed slugs carry a stale date, these dates are less sure, and they feed only the pool. Rows that are not shows but carry no Amendment 4 word are counted, as the rules say: Ballroom 1 flea row and a burlesque revue in S1, and 2 flea rows, a drag showcase and the Waterloo Arts Fest day (presenter line 'Outdoor Stage!') in S2; Tavern 1 brunch row in each period; each room still meets 20 / 20 without them (Ballroom 41 / 28, Tavern 63 / 34). The counts are lower bounds, not strict: the copies are 69 and 117 days apart, so shows announced and held between copies are missed unless a CDX slug gives them (without a room), and a cancellation after the last copy that lists a show does not show. Corrections to the 15 Oct record (Ballroom 44 / 32, Tavern 64 / 34, unknown 24 / 17, requests 17): the Ballroom row moved out of S1 was counted at its first date; the Tavern's 16 Sep row is now counted and noted; the This Way Out rows are a pool room of their own; the unknown-room figures could not be traced row by row and are replaced by the CDX-only counts above; requests.log has 18 lines for this venue.
- Coverage, compiled (18 Oct 2026, Amendment 12): the compiled `shows.csv` (220 rows: 169 cite an archive listing copy, 51 a live event page) gives Beachland Ballroom 49 / 38, Beachland Tavern 76 / 41, This Way Out 6 / 4 and the house room 0 / 0; building 131 / 83. The compile read 456 live own-site event pages /shows/<slug> through polite.py (18 Oct 2026, 18:59-19:22 UTC: 447 at 200, 9 at 404; no 403, 429 or bot check; web.archive.org 0). They give the 37 period shows known only from the CDX list a title, a date and a room, so none is left in the house room, whose 4 rows (S1 2, S2 2) are all `off-site`, and the house room's figures in the Coverage line above (23 / 13, the re-admission count; Beachland was re-admitted, not settled) no longer apply. The re-admission reading used live pages for date and room checks only (Read line above); this wider use is for the lead to confirm. Removed: S1 1 canceled (Chicago Farmer & the Fieldnotes, Tavern, 24 Apr, marked on its live page) and 2 off-site; S2 1 by the Amendment 4 words (Ballroom) and 2 off-site. The venue reuses Webflow items: the fayuca-09-jul page now holds a 9 Jul show at Treelawn Social Club, written `off-site`, and the Fayuca show of the 10 May copy is not written; the-starlet-strip-14-mar now holds a 25 Apr Ballroom show, which is written; two more pages changed billing and are written as the live pages show them (report.json, reading.live_replaces_copy, keeps the copy-side fields). No period night is a gap (the 2 Mar copy runs to 20 Oct), but the counts stay lower bounds. Ballroom and Tavern are test rooms and This Way Out and the house room are pool, as `venues.csv` has them.
- Presenters, compiled (18 Oct 2026): Beachland Ballroom, 9 of 87 counted rows name a presenter: BravoArtist 2, Ohio Burlesque 2, and 1 each for Outback (with BravoArtist, 17 Apr), Choir! Choir! Choir!, Porch Porch Productions, Grog Shop, Cleveland West SOR and Strongsville SOR (two School of Rock franchises); Beachland Tavern, 6 of 117: BravoArtist 4, EMB 1 and THE GROG SHOP 1; This Way Out, 0 of 10. The live pages' presenter lines were overwritten after the shows and the latest reading wins, so 5 period rows that a copy shows as presented have an empty presented_by (8 Apr, Beachland with Grog Shop; 8 May, BravoArtist; 2 Jun and 28 Jul, Grog Shop; 29 Sep, BravoArtist; report.json lists them under live_replaces_copy for the literal reading); this is why Grog Shop has 1 counted Ballroom row here against 4 in the Operator line above (the 8 Apr, 2 Jun and 28 Jul rows lost it). No company presents more than half of a room's rows, so there is no house booker and the dry run lists none. No LN/AEG presenter by the presenter check: Outback is Outback Presents, in which AEG's reported stake is a minority one, so the verdict is no; Porch Porch Productions and EMB were not identified. 'Beachland' is named only on an `off-site` row.
- Capacity: Ballroom 500, Tavern 148 (https://www.beachlandballroom.com/about); none published for This Way Out or the house room.
- Also checked: The Cleveland metro (Cleveland, OH MSA, OMB Bulletin 23-01) has one eligible venue of another cluster, Music Box Supper Club (settled eligible on 17 Oct 2026; in-house booking, no link to the Beachland's owners or presenters), so §5 passes; the pass is symmetric, and each venue passes §5 only through the other. Cleveland is not in the Minneapolis-St Paul metro, and the Beachland is neither Hideout nor Black Cat (§6, Amendment 11). The row was re-admitted on §5 on 18 Oct 2026, after a second check under the 16 Oct readings.
- Requests during the eligibility check: www.beachlandballroom.com 13 (7 at 200, 6 at 404), web.archive.org 5 (2 CDX lists, 3 listing copies), freshwatercleveland.com 1 (robots.txt only); all on 14 Oct 2026 between 20:20 and 20:44 UTC (requests.log). No request was made for this second check.

### Music Box Supper Club (`cle-musicbox-*`, Cleveland)
- Source: The venue's own WordPress HTML: /schedule/ (paginated list with date, time, title and a separate room line), /schedule/at-a-glance-concert-schedule/ (a table of every announced show with day, M/D, time, a room-code column and the title, with a 'Presented by' subtitle on some rows) and single /event/<slug>/ pages ('Venue:', 'Showtime:', 'Doors open:'). The live site lists only coming shows and past event posts are taken off it (the event sitemap's 111 URLs are almost all Oct 2026 onward; its 4 Apr-Sep slugs are older or later strays), so S1/S2 come from web.archive.org copies of the own listing pages. The WP REST API gives no events route.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/20260412000330id_/https://musicboxcle.com/schedule/ ; https://web.archive.org/web/20260416172553id_/https://musicboxcle.com/schedule/ ; https://web.archive.org/web/20260418004042id_/https://musicboxcle.com/schedule/page/2/ ; https://web.archive.org/web/20260517235106id_/https://musicboxcle.com/schedule/at-a-glance-concert-schedule/ ; CDX queries for musicboxcle.com/schedule/ (2 captures), /schedule/page/2/ (1), /schedule/page/3/ (0) and /schedule/at-a-glance-concert-schedule/ (1), all four picked; live (15-16 Oct 2026, for dates, terms and operator only, no S1/S2 row): https://musicboxcle.com/ , /schedule/ , /schedule/page/11/ , /schedule/at-a-glance-concert-schedule/ , /event-sitemap.xml , /event/duprees-dead-band-oct15/ , /event/slow-roll-cleveland-oct5/ , /about/ , /schedule/booking-info/ , /seating-charts/ , /faqs/ , /private-events/ , /privacy-policy/ , /wp-json/ ; and, for the record's search for Apr-Sep rows only, /sitemap_index.xml , /events-sitemap.xml and /?s=brunch (upcoming shows only)
- Dates checked: Live listing 'Sun, Oct 18 / 7:00 pm / Carbon Leaf / Concert Hall' matches the event slug carbon-leaf-oct18 and its Etix link. Event page duprees-dead-band-oct15: 'Thursday 15 Oct ... Venue: Supper Club Showtime: 7:30 pm Doors open: 6:00 pm'. Event page slow-roll-cleveland-oct5: 'Monday 05 Oct ... Venue: Concert Hall Showtime: 6:30 pm', published 2026-03-31, so the listed date is the show date, not the publish date. The copies give month and day only; the year comes from the capture date, and every one of the 147 rows read fits its 2026 weekday (0 mismatches). The independent check confirmed 8 random rows in their source bodies, among them Supper Club 19 Apr 'CSNY Brunch' (16 Apr copy), Concert Hall 26 Apr 'Beatles Brunch' (schedule page 2, 17 Apr) and Supper Club 2 Aug 'Reggae Sundays: Umojah Nation' (At-A-Glance, 17 May). Starts run from 12:00 (Sunday brunch shows) to 21:30; none falls between 00:01 and 04:59 (Amendment 3).
- robots.txt (15-16 Oct 2026): allowed. https://musicboxcle.com/robots.txt (Yoast) is 'User-agent: * / Disallow:' (allows everything), plus one stray rule 'Disallow: /wp-content/uploads/vfb/' before any group. There is no Anthropic group. polite.py allowed every venue page read, and web.archive.org on 17 Oct.
- Terms (15-16 Oct 2026): copyright-only. Footer on every page: '© 2026 Music Box Supper Club 1148 Main Avenue Cleveland, OH 44113 All Rights Reserved.', a bare notice (Amendment 14). There is no terms page. The privacy policy has no use licence and no clause on copying, reproducing, personal-only use or automated access; its only 'may not use' sentence is about service providers' use of customers' personal data. The independent check scanned every saved body and the four copies and found no copy, reproduce, republish, personal-use, scraping or automated-access wording. Checked: https://musicboxcle.com/ (footer); https://musicboxcle.com/terms (404); https://musicboxcle.com/terms/ (404); https://musicboxcle.com/terms-of-use/ (404); https://musicboxcle.com/terms-of-service/ (404); https://musicboxcle.com/terms-and-conditions/ (404); https://musicboxcle.com/legal/ (404); https://musicboxcle.com/privacy-policy/; https://musicboxcle.com/faqs/; https://musicboxcle.com/about/
- Platform: self-hosted WordPress with a custom theme ('clubber', footer 'Website by' Zwinggi Creative). Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Webflow, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer in the live pages or in any of the four copies. Etix is used for ticket links only. The only 'powered by' text is 'Private Event Software powered by Tripleseat' on /private-events/, a booking-form vendor. Shield Security closes the WP REST wp/v2 routes to anonymous users: /wp-json/wp/v2/media answered 401 ('shield_block_anon_restapi'). The independent check corrected the first record here: the /wp-json/ index itself answered 200, and it has no events, tribe or calendar route, so there is no feed to read either way. Google reCAPTCHA v3 is loaded only for the contact and newsletter forms. No bot check and no 403: every venue request returned 200 or 404 apart from that one 401, and all four copies are full venue pages.
- Operator (§1, Amendment 14): Owner: an employee ownership trust. The first record named the 2014 structure (two private owners as managing partners with more than 20 investors, including the Jacobs Group, which owned the building; https://www.clevescene.com/news/industry-vets-to-open-ambitious-flats-music-club-3631687/ and https://freshwatercleveland.com/features/themusicbox082213.aspx). The independent check corrected it: March 2026 press (https://www.crainscleveland.com/arts-entertainment/ccl-music-box-forms-eot-20260306/ and https://hoodline.com/2026/03/flats-favorite-music-box-supper-club-hands-keys-to-the-staff/) and the venue's own staff page (https://musicboxcle.com/staff2/, seen only as a search snippet) report that in Feb/Mar 2026 a controlling interest went into an employee ownership trust, led by the founding owner (a private owner), and all 34 outside investors were bought out; the reason given was to keep local control. The correction is used here. House booker: the venue itself. Its booking page asks artists to send links to its own booking address ('If you're an artist interested in playing the Music Box Supper Club, please send links to your website and a live video to [email image]', https://musicboxcle.com/schedule/booking-info/), and 2014 press says a co-owner books the bands (https://www.clevescene.com/music/dinner-and-a-show-new-music-venue-aims-to-enhance-the-cleveland-music-scene-4331171/, search snippet). Ticketing is Etix (https://musicboxcle.com/faqs/), not Ticketmaster or AXS (the FAQ keeps leftover AXS wording only inside commented-out text). No public source names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake, house booker or talent-buying deal. Outside presenters on counted S1/S2 rows ('Presented by' on the At-A-Glance copy, or 'presents' in the title): 8 of 133 rows. Get Away With Murder, Inc. presents 2 (Supper Club, 1 in S1 and 1 in S2); The Cleveland Academy of Poetry & Letters, Tropical Cleveland, AFJTB and Cleveland Jazz Orchestra 1 each (Concert Hall); 3 Birds and The Wire and Vanity Crash 1 each (Supper Club). That is 4 of 68 Concert Hall rows and 4 of 65 Supper Club rows. Two of these presenter lines also name a private person, who is counted and not written; `presented_by` on `shows.csv` leaves those persons out. None is Live Nation or AEG and none presents more than half of a room's shows, so none is a house booker and none joins a cluster.
- Rooms: the room comes from a field of its own, never from the title: p.event-arc-venue on the schedule pages ('Supper Club' / 'Concert Hall') and the At-A-Glance code column, read with the page's own legend (SC Supper Club, CH Concert Hall, MB Music Box Full Venue, RT Rooftop Deck, RF Riverfront Porch, PDR Private Dining Room). Supper Club is the downstairs stage and Concert Hall the upstairs one ('the building offers two stages- a downstairs Supper Club and an upstairs Concert Hall- and a restaurant', https://musicboxcle.com/about/). Only SC and CH occur in the S1/S2 rows, so no row goes to a house room; a row coded MB, RT, RF or PDR, or with no room, would go to a pool room of the building.
- Coverage: settled on 17-18 Oct 2026 from saved copies, after web.archive.org was stopped for good in the fetcher during the 16-17 Oct check (second 429, 00:38 on 17 Oct server time), which had left the venue undecided. The four copies were fetched on 17 Oct under the 30 s archive rule and read offline; an independent check recounted them. Lower bounds: Supper Club S1 >=39, S2 >=26; Concert Hall S1 >=34, S2 >=34; building S1 >=73, S2 >=60. The independent check's recount matches these counts. The copies hold 147 rows (10 + 10 + 10 + 117); a union by event slug, latest copy winning, leaves 140, and 134 of them fall on local nights from 1 Apr to 30 Sep 2026 (11 Apr to 25 Sep). Raw rows: Supper Club 40 / 26, Concert Hall 34 / 34 (74 / 60). Removed by the Amendment 4 words: S1 1 (Supper Club 24 Apr 'Drag Bingo', bingo). No row is marked canceled ('event-cancel-out' holds only 'Sold Out' / 'Free Entry'), none is off-site and none is untitled. Rows that carry no Amendment 4 word count, among them 2 Murder Mystery Dinner Party rows in the Supper Club. S1: 23 raw rows from the three schedule copies and 51 from the At-A-Glance copy; S2: all 60 from the At-A-Glance copy of 17 May. Gaps: no copy covers 1-10 Apr or 3-16 May (2 May is only partly seen). At the seam between the 16 Apr page 1 (ends 24 Apr) and the 17 Apr page 2 (starts 25 Apr) one row may be hidden; the settle report says at most one, and the independent check notes that more could be if shows were added between those two captures. S2 holds only what was announced by 17 May (September is thin: Concert Hall 7, Supper Club 1), and cancellations made after 17 May cannot be seen: Supper Club S2 (26) would fall below 20 only if 7 or more of its listed Jul-Sep shows were later canceled. The CDX lists no other capture of the four listing URLs; /event/<slug>/ captures were not queried. Both rooms meet coverage by themselves, so both are test rooms of one cluster (`cle-musicbox`); `shows.csv` confirms or corrects this (Amendment 12).
- Coverage, compiled (18 Oct 2026, Amendment 12): the compiled `shows.csv` (134 rows from the four saved copies; no request) gives Concert Hall 34 / 34 and Supper Club 39 / 26 (written rows 40 / 26: 'Drag Bingo', 24 Apr, has an empty drop_reason and is left out of the count by its word); building 73 / 60. The 134 rows match the settle `shows.csv` one to one on room, date, start, title and source copy, and presented_by matches on all 8 presenter rows. 0 canceled, 0 off-site, 0 untitled. Gaps as above: no capture for 1-10 Apr or 3-16 May, 24-25 Apr and 2 May only partly seen, and S2 only as announced by 17 May; every capture of the four listing URLs in the CDX was used, and /event/<slug>/ captures were never queried. Both rooms are test rooms, as `venues.csv` has them.
- Presenters, compiled (18 Oct 2026): 8 of 133 counted rows, as in the settle: Concert Hall 4 of 68 (The Cleveland Academy of Poetry & Letters, Tropical Cleveland, AFJTB and Cleveland Jazz Orchestra, 1 each), Supper Club 4 of 65 (Get Away With Murder, Inc. 2, 3 Birds and The Wire 1, Vanity Crash 1). Two presenter lines (Supper Club 16 Jul, Concert Hall 11 Sep) also name a private person, who is not written. No house booker (the dry run lists none) and no LN/AEG presenter: the presenter check finds none of the others Live Nation or AEG and did not identify The Cleveland Academy of Poetry & Letters.
- §5 and §6: the Cleveland metro is the 'Cleveland, OH' MSA of OMB Bulletin 23-01 (formerly Cleveland-Elyria); not Study 1 and not an Amendment 11 room. Beachland Ballroom & Tavern (`cle-beachland-*`), another cluster in the same metro, was 'not used' in `candidates.md` on §5 alone; it is re-admitted on §5 on 18 Oct 2026 through this venue (its own section gives its counts), so §5 passes here, and each venue passes §5 only through the other.
- Capacity: no figure on the venue's own pages (the seating charts are images only; the private-events page gives no numbers). Figures on outside listings and in 2014 press are not the venue's own and were not used (Amendment 8: size unknown).
- Programme: mostly music (national and local touring acts, many tribute acts and Sunday tribute brunch shows), with some dinner theatre and other nights. This is only a description; nothing was filtered by it.
- Requests during the eligibility check: musicboxcle.com 27 (requests.log: 4 on 15 Oct and 23 on 16 Oct 2026 UTC; 2 of them robots.txt reads; 20 at 200, 6 at 404 (the terms paths) and 1 at 401 (/wp-json/wp/v2/media)), web.archive.org 8 on 17 Oct 2026 UTC (19:31-19:35: 4 CDX queries and the 4 copies, all 200) plus 1 CDX refusal logged on 16 Oct 2026 UTC (20:29) while the archive was stopped. The settle and its check made no request.
- Note for compiling: `shows.csv` leaves `drop_reason` empty on the Supper Club row of 24 Apr ('Drag Bingo'). The word 'bingo' takes it out of coverage either way (Amendment 12 counts rows with none of the Amendment 4 words), which gives Supper Club S1 39; a count by `drop_reason` alone gives 40. In the run it is dropped as `non-music` only if the word lies outside the resolved name (Amendment 4).

### ALICE (`cph-alice`, Copenhagen)
- Source: The venue's own past-event archive (https://alicecph.com/event/, 'Alle datoer' month filter), loaded by the site's own GET call to admin-ajax.php, one page per month (data-max-pages=1). Event pages carry schema.org Event JSON-LD with startDate; there is also an event sitemap. The live site is the source; web.archive.org was not needed.
- Read: https://alicecph.com/wp/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php?action=component&component=EventList&type=past&pagination=1&id=EventArchiveList&month=MM&year=2026 for MM = 04..09, and event pages https://alicecph.com/event/<slug>/ for date and location checks
- Dates checked: The Necks: list 14.04.26 = page 'Koncerten starter kl' time = JSON-LD startDate 2026-04-14T20:00+02:00. Bobo & Behaja: 11.07.26 = 2026-07-11T21:00+02:00. Cole Pulice: 30.04.26 = 2026-04-30T20:00+02:00. Juana Molina 09.04.26 and BCUC 28.05.26 also match, and the nine pages opened in the second check fall on their listed dates (+02:00). The dates are show dates, not publish dates.
- robots.txt (fetched 15 Oct 2026, applied to every read on 16 Oct): allowed. robots.txt: 'User-agent: * / Disallow: /wp/wp-admin/ / Allow: /wp/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php', with a sitemap; no Anthropic group. polite.py allowed every venue URL read. No bot check and no 403 (every response was 200 or an ordinary 404).
- Terms (16 Oct 2026): silent (no terms of use; nothing on reuse). The only terms page covers purchases ('Disse salgs- og leveringsbetingelser indeholder de vilkår, som gælder for køb af billetter, medlemskaber og eventuelle andre produkter købt gennem vores hjemmeside'); its only copy clause is about tickets ('Billetter må ikke kopieres eller på anden vis anvendes flere gange'). The privacy policy has no use-licence or reuse section; the conduct policy covers harassment and discrimination only; the press page invites use of its press photos with credit. The footer has no ©/'All rights reserved' line. No text forbids copying, reuse or automated reading, or limits use to personal viewing. Checked: https://alicecph.com/ (footer: Adfærdspolitik, Salgs- og leveringsbetingelser, Privatlivspolitik); https://alicecph.com/salgs-og-leveringsbetingelser/; https://alicecph.com/privatlivspolitik/; https://alicecph.com/adfaerdspolitik/; https://alicecph.com/pressemateriale/; https://alicecph.com/faq/; https://alicecph.com/om-alice/; https://alicecph.com/kontaktinfo/; https://alicecph.com/terms/ (404); https://alicecph.com/terms-of-use/ (404)
- Independence: run by Fonden ALICE, a foundation (CVR 3912 7318) formed when the venues Global and Jazzhouse merged on 1 Feb 2018; a regional venue ('Regionalt Spillested' 2025-2028) supported by Statens Kunstfond, Københavns Kommune, AP Møller Fonden and Augustinus Fonden. House booking: 'Koncertprogrammet på ALICE bookes af ALICEs Musikgruppe' (a group of four bookers listed on the venue's contact page; booking requests to booking@alicecph.com). Event JSON-LD names ALICE as organizer and tickets are sold on the venue's own Tixly shop. No public source names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake or booker. Outside presenters (KLANG Festival, Copenhagen Jazz Festival) are partners on a minority of nights; KLANG presents 7 of the 43 counted S1 rows.
- Rooms: one. There is no room field or room filter (the only filters are genre and month); event pages say only 'Siddende' or 'Stående' (seated or standing). Capacity: none published by the venue (not on om-alice, faq or pressemateriale); room size unknown.
- Coverage: S1 43, S2 30 (all rows of the own month lists for Apr-Sep 2026: 46 and 34 listed, minus 2 canceled (AFLYST) S1 rows, 1 off-site S1 row (Christians Kirke, in the teaser and on the opened page) and 4 off-site S2 rows (Sønderho and Bellevue Teatret from the teasers, Konservatoriets Koncertsal from the title, Kildevæld Kulturcenter from the opened page)). No row holds an Amendment 4 word. The JSON-LD location reads ALICE / Nørre Allé 7 even for off-site rows, so off-site rows are found only in the page text: at least 28 S1 rows (13 teasers that place the night at ALICE or in its hall, plus 15 opened pages that name no other building) and 23 S2 rows (10 teasers plus 13 opened pages) are confirmed on-site; 'Athelas Sinfonietta x ALICE' names ALICE only as a partner in the title and is not counted as confirmed, so the counts are upper bounds and the role holds even if every unopened row were off-site.
- Platform: Self-hosted WordPress 6.3.2 (custom theme alice2020); ticketing on Tixly (billet.alicecph.com, not read). Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or a Rockhouse/Etix site.
- Requests during the eligibility check: alicecph.com 56 (4 in the 15 Oct archive survey, 40 in the full check and 12 in the second check on 16 Oct; see requests.log), web.archive.org 2 (two CDX index queries for alicecph.com on 16 Oct, at 20:09 and 21:21 UTC; no snapshot was read. The second query was first refused by polite.py at 20:18 UTC, while archive reads were stopped, and was sent at 21:21 after reads resumed; it is not a third query)

### Amager Bio & BETA (`cph-amagerbio-*`, Copenhagen)
- Source: The venue's own homepage concert calendar (https://ab-b.dk/), read from web.archive.org copies. Each concert card (div.mix concert) gives a data-scene field (the room), a data-status field, the day and month, a 'Show' time, the title and a /k/<slug>/ link; the year is taken from the capture date. The live site keeps only coming shows: past /k/<slug>/ pages return 404, concert-sitemap.xml lists 88 mostly future slugs and the WordPress REST root /api/ exposes no concert type. amagerbio.dk redirects to ab-b.dk. Two scouting records, one for the building (`amagerbio-dk`) and one for the BETA stage alone (`ab-b-dk`), describe this one building and are merged here.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://ab-b.dk/ for ts 20260307111054, 20260308080135, 20260412095110, 20260416013429, 20260515230102, 20260523235610, 20260615003355, 20260623193401, 20260822235718, 20260827075706, 20260920093023 (11 homepage copies, all used; 3 stored gzip-compressed, 71 to 92 cards each); https://web.archive.org/web/20260312225158id_/https://ab-b.dk/scene/beta/ (a 'Beta Arkiv' page with 0 concert cards, not a listing, not counted); the CDX lists of ab-b.dk/ (16 captures, 7 Mar to 20 Sep 2026), ab-b.dk/scene/beta/ (1) and the /k/ prefix (282 URLs). Live https://ab-b.dk/k/hyd-us/ and https://ab-b.dk/k/sigh/ (404) and the about, contact, privacy and terms paths below, for the date, operator and terms checks only.
- Dates checked: These are show dates. Live https://ab-b.dk/k/hyd-us/ reads 'HYD - Beta - Tirsdag 27. oktober', matching its card (27 okt, scene Beta). The 15 May copy's carousel gives 'Mandag 18. maj | Steve Hackett' with 'Dørene åbner kl. 19:00' and the card says 18 maj; 18 May 2026 is a Monday. The 23 Jun copy's carousel gives 'Fredag 4. december | Polyphia' and 'Fredag 20. november | Blood and Sun', and both weekdays fit 2026. Every card is labelled 'Show'; the counted rows start between 16:00 and 21:00 local time, so Amendment 3 moves no date.
- robots.txt (15 Oct 2026 UTC): allowed. https://ab-b.dk/robots.txt (Yoast block): 'User-agent: * / Disallow:' with no other rule and no Anthropic group; amagerbio.dk's robots.txt is the same. polite.py allowed every venue URL read; web.archive.org allowed the snapshot and CDX paths. No bot check and no 403 in any live page or copy; the only captcha text is the newsletter's reCAPTCHA widget.
- Terms (15-16 Oct 2026 UTC; re-checked on the saved copies 18 Oct): none-found. There is no terms page: https://ab-b.dk/terms, /terms-of-use, /terms-of-service, /terms-and-conditions and /legal return 404. The footer links only 'Forsiden FAQ Find os Kontakt' and carries no copyright line. The privacy pages (https://ab-b.dk/privatlivspolitik/ and the two social-media policies) cover personal data only, with no use licence and no ban on copying; https://ab-b.dk/praktisk-information/ covers house rules and cameras; the other 'betingelser' pages are old competition terms (2018, 2023). Also checked: https://ab-b.dk/, https://ab-b.dk/in-english/, https://ab-b.dk/om-amagerbio-2/, https://ab-b.dk/kontakt/, https://ab-b.dk/page-sitemap.xml. The only 'terms' string in the copies is a utmTerms cookie setting.
- Platform: self-hosted WordPress 7.0.7 with Yoast SEO, its own theme (wp-content/themes/ab2016), a custom post type 'concert' and a 'scene' taxonomy. Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue, Webflow or Rockhouse Partners: no 'Powered by' footer and no Rockhouse or Etix text in any copy. Tickets are sold through Ticketmaster under the venue's own brands (brand=dk_amagerbio, dk_beta, dk_at, dk_atl); some cards carry brand=dk_livenation.
- Operator (§1, Amendment 14): Kulturbegivenhedsfonden Odd Productions (CVR 34608555), a foundation set up by Roskilde Festival and VEGA, runs the venue on a municipal concession from 1 Sep 2025 (six years, with an option for two more); Copenhagen Municipality has held the right of use of the building since 1989 (https://ab-b.dk/om-amagerbio-2/: 'Vinderen af udbuddet blev Kulturbegivenhedsfonden Odd Productions - en fond etableret af Roskilde Festival og spillestedet VEGA i fællesskab'; the staff moved over to Odd Productions; https://ab-b.dk/privatlivspolitik/ gives 'Odd Productions - CVR: 34608555 - Øresundsvej 6'). Booking is in house: the contact page lists a staff booker under 'Booking og PR' with an @ab-b.dk address (a private person, not named here), and the English page says the staff of 10 are 'responsible for booking'. No public source found names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake or house booker. The only outside presenter named in the copies is Copenhagen Metal Fest, on 1 S1 row (4 Apr, Amager Bio), and only in its slug. Live Nation appears only as brand=dk_livenation on Ticketmaster links, on 9 of the 49 S1 rows of the settle count (Amager Bio 6, Beta 3) and on no counted S2 row; a ticket link is not a presenter, so §1 passes. `venues.csv` gives the operator Odd Productions and the cluster `odd-productions`, apart from `cph-vega`: VEGA co-founded the foundation (press snippets also say VEGA's director sits on its board), but no source read names VEGA as owner or booker of Amager Bio & BETA. All rooms of both clusters are pool, so reading them as one cluster would not change the cap.
- Rooms: the card's data-scene field ('Amager Bio' or 'Beta'), which the scene-ab / scene-beta class and the card's venue label always agree with (857 cards); the site also has a scene taxonomy (/scene/ab/, /scene/beta/). Cards with no scene go to the house room `cph-amagerbio-house` (21 of the 857 cards; in the counted rows, RUSK x BETA #3 and Copenhagen Metal Fest dag 1 and dag 2), even when the title names BETA (Amendment 14, rooms). Capacity: Amager Bio 1,250 standing and BETA 200 standing (about 82 seated), the figures the venue publishes (https://ab-b.dk/in-english/, https://ab-b.dk/om-amagerbio-2/).
- Coverage: Amager Bio S1 >=22, S2 >=10; Beta S1 >=25, S2 >=8; house S1 >=1, S2 >=2; building S1 >=48, S2 >=20 (lower bounds; settled in round 2 on 18 Oct 2026 from the saved copies, with no request of its own). Rows are the union by slug of the 11 homepage copies, the latest copy winning, 70 rows. Canceled 1 (Hiraes, Beta, 24 Aug, status 'aflyst' in the 22 Aug copy). Off-site 1: M.O.P. (Amager Bio, 24 May), whose badge in the 23 May copy reads 'Flyttet til Loppen' (moved to Loppen, another building); the settle report counted it (Amager Bio S1 23, building 49), and the independent recount, which drops it as off-site under Amendment 1, is used here. Moved 1: 'Dysgnostic - albumrelease' was listed for 20 Jun and then for 15 Aug under the same slug, so it counts once, in S2. Amendment 4 words: 0. No room meets 20 / 20 by itself (S2 10, 8 and 2), so every room is pool and the venue is pool only (Amendment 14, pool-only venues); the rooms together meet coverage. The S2 margin is 0: S2 includes Syd (9 Sep) and USO (11 Sep), both Amager Bio, listed in the 15 Jun and 23 Jun copies and missing from the 22 Aug and 27 Aug copies without an 'aflyst' or 'udskudt' mark, while the site does mark cancellations elsewhere. Under the written rule (minus anything marked canceled) they count; read as canceled, the building has S2 18 and fails §4. S2 also includes two non-music rows that no Amendment 4 word removes (Nordic Elite Wrestling IV, 5 Sep; Sam Morril, a stand-up act, 13 Sep) and the two house rows of Copenhagen Metal Fest (18-19 Sep). The ab-b.dk/ CDX has no capture between 23 Jun and 22 Aug, none between 27 Aug and 20 Sep and none after 20 Sep up to 15 Oct; the five unused captures (4 Apr, 12 May, 23 May 20:57, 7 Jun, 12 Jun) can change only S1; the /k/ prefix CDX has no event slug missing from the union. So the S2 counts are all the archive can give. July is the venue's summer break.
- Coverage, compiled (18 Oct 2026, Amendment 12): the compiled `shows.csv` (71 rows; the compile read the 11 saved homepage copies and one /scene/beta/ copy, which holds no concert card, and the rows cite 10 of the homepage copies; no request) gives Amager Bio 23 / 10, BETA 25 / 8 and the house room 1 / 2; building 49 / 20. One row more than the settle: Danser med Drenge (Amager Bio, 9 May), listed in the 7 Mar to 16 Apr copies, whose slug the 20 Sep copy reuses for an 8 May 2027 show, so the settle's union by slug had lost it. Removed: 1 canceled (Hiraes, BETA, 24 Aug), 1 off-site (M.O.P., Amager Bio, 24 May, 'Flyttet til Loppen', written `off-site` with canceled 0), 0 by the Amendment 4 words. The S2 margin is still 0 and still includes Syd (9 Sep) and USO (11 Sep), which leave later copies with no cancel mark (Randall King, 6 May, does the same in S1); they are written with canceled 0 from their last listing. 'Dysgnostic - albumrelease' is one row, 15 Aug. Under a 14-day freshness reading (a night counts only if a copy taken at most 14 days before it lists it), 88 of the 183 nights are gaps; no capture exists for 1-3 Apr, 1-11 May, 8 Jul-22 Aug and 11-19 Sep, and 5 S1 captures were not prefetched (the five unused captures of the Coverage line above; report.json's more_captures note gives 5, its list names 4 of them). Every room stays pool, as `venues.csv` has it. The merge draft's `venues.csv` now gives Amager Bio 1,250 and BETA 200, the figures the venue publishes, so the dry run sizes Amager Bio large and BETA small (Amendment 8).
- Presenters, compiled (18 Oct 2026): Copenhagen Metal Fest on 1 of the 69 counted rows (Amager Bio, 4 Apr), named only in the slug of the linked show page (copenhagen-metal-fest-presents-a-copenhagen-metal-night); whether a slug counts as the listing naming the presenter is left to the lead. No other presenter, no house booker (the dry run lists none) and no LN/AEG presenter: the presenter check finds Copenhagen Metal Fest a festival organiser with no Live Nation or AEG link.
- §5 and §6: the Copenhagen metro (OECD-EU functional urban area of København) has eligible venues of other clusters in `venues.csv` (`cph-alice`, `cph-loppen`, `cph-pumpehuset`, `cph-vega`), so §5 passes. Copenhagen is not in Study 1, and the venue is neither Hideout nor Black Cat. As a pool-only venue it adds no test cluster and does not move the cap (ALICE stays the 20th test cluster); its rows feed the Copenhagen pool.
- Note for compiling: rows come from the settle rows (venue_id `ab-b-dk:<room>`; Amager Bio to `cph-amagerbio-bio`, Beta to `cph-amagerbio-beta`, house to `cph-amagerbio-house`). Its M.O.P. row (24 May) still has an empty drop_reason and must be written `off-site`; the compile wrote it so. The Coverage counts above are settle counts; the compiled counts are in 'Coverage, compiled'.
- Requests during the eligibility check: ab-b.dk 29 (requests.log: 5 on 15 Oct 2026 UTC, robots.txt included; 24 on 16 Oct, 16 answered 200 and 8 404, among them the terms paths and /k/sigh/), amagerbio.dk 2 (15 Oct: robots.txt and the redirect), web.archive.org 30 naming ab-b.dk (3 on 15 Oct: 2 CDX queries and 1 copy; 2 on 16 Oct: 1 CDX query and 1 copy; 24 on 17 Oct by the prefetch, 17:03-17:15 UTC: 3 CDX queries and 21 copy reads, since the tasks for both records queued 10 homepage copies each, plus the scene/beta copy; 1 on 18 Oct, 18:29 UTC, the 22 Aug copy), plus 4 refusals logged without sending (3 on 16 Oct while the archive was paused or stopped, 1 on 18 Oct during a 503 pause). All answered requests to the archive got 200.

### Loppen (`cph-loppen`, Copenhagen)
- Source: The venue's own HTML listing of past shows by year (/tidligere/2026, 10 rows per page, ?page=N; each row gives weekday, date, doors time, price and title), with /kalender/<slug> event pages that carry an iCal link (/node/N/field_event_date/addtocal.ics). The event pages have no schema.org JSON-LD.
- Read: https://loppen.dk/tidligere/2026 and https://loppen.dk/tidligere/2026?page=1 ... ?page=6 (7 pages, 70 rows, 15 Oct back to 20 Mar 2026, dates continuous across page boundaries); event pages https://loppen.dk/kalender/<slug> for date and location checks
- Dates checked: The listed dates are show dates. Cory Hanson: listing 17 Sep 2026, event page 'Bemærk at koncerten er rykket fra den 6. februar til den 17. september 2026'. Bozenga: listing 11 Jul 2026, page 'når Loppen får besøg af ... den 11. juli'. The Smith Street Band: listing 20 Jun 2026, page 'Vi ses den 20. juni'. Nausia x Svin: listing 12 Jul 2026 17.00, page 'coheadliner på Loppen den 12. juli'. Listed times (19.00 to 21.00, one 12.00 and one 17.00) are doors times: /info says 'Music starts approximately 1 hour after doors open'. No start falls between 00:01 and 04:59.
- robots.txt (15 Oct 2026): allowed. Stock Drupal robots.txt: 'User-agent: *' with 'Crawl-delay: 10', disallowing only Drupal system paths (/includes/, /misc/, /modules/, /profiles/, /scripts/, /themes/, install and similar files). No Anthropic group. polite.py allowed /, /tidligere/2026, /kalender/*, /info and /kontakt; the 10 s delay was honoured.
- Terms (16 Oct 2026): none-found. No terms of use, no © line and no text limiting copying, reuse or automated access on any page read. The 'Persondatapolitik' link on /info points back to http://www.loppen.dk, the home page. Checked: https://loppen.dk/ (footer: only CHRISTIANIA, DANSK LIVE and Københavns Kommune links); https://loppen.dk/info ('Officielle links og dokumenter': statutes and annual reports); https://loppen.dk/kontakt; https://loppen.dk/terms (404); https://loppen.dk/terms-of-use (404); https://loppen.dk/terms-of-service (404); https://loppen.dk/terms-and-conditions (404); https://loppen.dk/legal (404); https://loppen.dk/persondatapolitik (404); https://loppen.dk/handelsbetingelser (404)
- Platform: Self-hosted Drupal (drupal.js, Drupal.settings, /sites/all/ theme on its own host), not Squarespace, Wix, Webflow, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Rockhouse Partners/Etix. Ticket links go out to eventim-light.com and shop.paylogic.com; neither hosts the site, and no ticket page is read.
- Operator (§1): Musikforeningen Loppen (a members' music association, CVR 10303702, Christiania; https://loppen.dk/kontakt and /info), supported by Statens Kunstfond and Københavns Kommune. Booking is in house (the venue's own booking office at loppen@loppen.dk on /kontakt). Web searches found no Live Nation or AEG owner, stake or house booker. Ticketing is eventim-light.com and Paylogic, neither of them Live Nation or AEG. Outside presenters credited in titles present a show or two each; 'FRIGJORT PRESENTS' is a festival started by Loppen's own volunteers.
- Rooms: one. A single concert hall (1st floor, Sydområdet 4B, Christiania); the listing and the event pages have no room field or room filter. Capacity 400, the venue's own figure (/info, 'Venue specifications: ... Capacity: 400.').
- Coverage: S1 34, S2 21 (every dated row on the 7 pages, counted at local dates, recounted by a second parser). S1: 37 rows, minus 2 marked '[AFLYST!]' (21 Apr, 11 Jun) and 1 removed by Amendment 4 (21 Jun 'FLEA MARKET': market). S2: 21 rows, none canceled, empty or word-matched. '[FLYTTET FRA AMAGER BIO]' (24 May) moved to Loppen and counts; Cory Hanson, moved from 6 Feb to 17 Sep ('OBS. - NY DATO!'), counts on its new date; read as postponed and dropped, S2 would be 20. The three Frigjort Festival nights (6-8 Aug) belong to a festival held at several Christiania locations, but each of their event pages heads its line-up 'LINE-UP // Loppen', so they are on-site; read as off-site, S2 would be 18.
- Requests during the eligibility check: loppen.dk 26 (2 during scouting on 15 Oct: robots.txt and /tidligere/2026; 24 during the eligibility check on 16 Oct), web.archive.org 2 (a CDX query and one copy of the home page during scouting, not used for counting)

### The Castle Hotel (`man-castle`, Manchester)
- Source: Venue's own HTML listings. /events/ shows only upcoming events (12 per page, paginated). The theme's own date filter /events/?date=DD/MM/YYYY (from jquery.main.js onSelect) returns the listing for any exact past date. There is no events REST route (the event post type is not in wp-json) and no iCal feed. Yoast event-sitemap1-3 list event URLs but carry no show dates. web.archive.org has copies of /events/ (2026-02-10, 2026-04-22) and of the homepage (nearest to 2026-06-01, 07-05, 08-01, 09-10). The CDX index was flaky (503 or timeout twice).
- Read: https://thecastlehotel.info/events/?date=DD/MM/YYYY ; https://thecastlehotel.info/events/ ; https://web.archive.org/web/20260422053418id_/https://thecastlehotel.info/events/ ; https://web.archive.org/web/2026MMDD000000id_/https://thecastlehotel.info/
- Dates checked: Event pages show the show date, the show time and a price: /events/gresley/ '05 05 2026 07.00 pm ... £14 Buy Tickets'; /events/theo-kandel/ '22 07 2026 07.00 pm ... £15'; /events/the-lotts/ '12 09 2026 07.00 pm ... £8'; /events/northcote/ '24 10 2026 07.00 pm'. Each matches its listing date. Note for the compiler: listing <time datetime='...T19:00Z'> writes local wall time with a false 'Z' (the page says 7.00 pm on a BST date), so read the time from the page, not as UTC. No evening date shifts either way. 'T00:00Z' rows are date-only.
- robots.txt (15 Oct 2026): allowed. robots.txt (Yoast block): 'User-agent: * / Disallow:' (empty), plus a Sitemap line. It has no AI-agent groups. polite.py allowed every path used.
- Terms (15 Oct 2026): none-found. No terms of use, no privacy page and no copyright notice anywhere on the site. The footer holds only 'Visit our sister venues:' with two logo links. /terms/ and /privacy-policy/ serve the homepage (canonical https://thecastlehotel.info/). The REST page list holds only Home, About, Find us, Access Information and TicketWeb Event. Checked: https://thecastlehotel.info/ (footer); https://thecastlehotel.info/terms/; https://thecastlehotel.info/privacy-policy/; https://thecastlehotel.info/wp-json/wp/v2/pages?per_page=100; https://thecastlehotel.info/page-sitemap.xml; https://thecastlehotel.info/about/; https://thecastlehotel.info/ticketweb-event/
- Platform: Self-hosted WordPress: custom theme "castle", /app/ paths, Yoast and Jetpack. It is not Fatsoma, Squarespace, Wix or Music Glue. Ticket buttons send buyers to external sellers (Skiddle, DICE, Fatsoma short links, WeGotTickets, Universe and others), but none of them hosts the site. A 2018 "TicketWeb Event" page still exists; it holds only an unrendered shortcode.
- Coverage: S1 >=26, S2 >=25 act-nights (lower bounds: days sampled through the site's own ?date= filter plus web.archive.org copies of /events/ and the home page). Rescheduled and canceled rows left out.
- Requests during the eligibility check: thecastlehotel.info 49, web.archive.org 10

- Operator: Now Wave, independent Manchester promoters, opened the venue in 2018 with a co-founder from live promotion (a private person, named in https://musicweek.com/live/read/we-re-proud-to-contribute-to-the-city-s-independent-scene-now-wave-open-new-manchester-venue-yes/073733: 'Independent promoters Now Wave are set to open a brand new four storey music, bar and food hangout in Manchester called Yes'; also https://www.manchestersfinest.com/music/now-wave-open-new-music-venue-yes-manchester/, seen in a search result; only its robots.txt was fetched, not the page). House-programmed: the only contact is the venue's own info address and no outside booker is named. The Now Wave homepage (https://www.nowwave.co.uk/) has no Live Nation, AEG or Academy Music Group ownership text; Now Wave also promotes shows at other Manchester rooms (Academy 2 among them) as an outside promoter, which does not decide §1. No public source found names a Live Nation or AEG stake in Now Wave or YES; the legal entity was not found at Companies House, so the owner's legal entity is not identified (§1 passes under reading 2). Ticket links go to See Tickets, DICE, Skiddle, RA and the festivals' own sites, with no Ticketmaster or AXS. The listing has no presenter field; outside presenters named in titles (DEF BY STEREO, ASHTYLR, CubCru, Body Talk and one other) present one row each.
- Rooms: the room field of each row, separate from the title: The Pink Room, Basement, Roof Terrace and Bar. The 4 rows given 'The Pink Room & Basement' (Manchester Punk Festival 3, 4 and 5 Apr; Manchester Psych Fest 5 Sep) go to the first room listed, the Pink Room. The 'Full Building' row (12 Sep) and the 2 rows whose room field holds only a time (29 Jun, 11 Jul) go to the house room `man-yes-house`.
- Coverage: S1 133, S2 77 (exact on the live month pages; rows the venue may have deleted when shows were canceled would not appear). By room: The Pink Room 64 / 30, Basement 56 / 37, Roof Terrace 12 / 7, Bar 0 / 1, house room 1 / 2. The Pink Room and Basement are test rooms; Roof Terrace, Bar and the house room are pool. No title is empty and none holds an Amendment 4 word, so none was removed; no row is marked canceled, postponed or moved, and none is off-site. The 7 World Cup 2026 screening rows (5 in S1, all in the Pink Room; 2 in S2, one in the Pink Room and one with no room, re-counted on the saved month pages) hold no Amendment 4 word and count; without them the Pink Room still has 59 / 29. 'Ultimate Power' is listed twice on 23 May (S1).
- Platform: custom hand-built site (Bootstrap 3, OwlCarousel, design credit to an independent web studio). No Fatsoma, Squarespace, Wix, Music Glue, Webflow or Weebly markers and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS' line, so no platform terms apply.
- Capacity: no figure published by the venue or Now Wave. Third-party guides give the Pink Room 250 and the Basement 60; these are not the venue's own figures and are not used.
- Requests during the eligibility check: www.yes-manchester.com 25, yes-manchester.com 4, web.archive.org 1, www.manchesterpunkfestival.co.uk 2, www.manchesterpsychfest.com 2, www.nowwave.co.uk 2, nowwave.co.uk 1, musicweek.com 2, www.musicweek.com 2, www.manchestersfinest.com 1
