Live pages read: https://ardmoremusichall.com/, /all-events/, /calendar/, /about/, /faqs/, /contact-us/, /private-events/, /private-event-downstairs/, /private-event-upstairs/, /wp-json/, /wp-json/wp/v2/events, /wp-sitemap.xml, /wp-sitemap-posts-page-1.xml, /page-sitemap.xml and the terms paths below; https://ardmoremusic.com/ and its /wp-json/wp/v2/events; operator page https://risingsunpresents.com/about.
- Dates checked: These are show dates. In the 12 May copy: The Joy Formidable, card 'Wed May 13', description 'Wednesday, May 13, 2026, Show: 7:00 PM', JSON-LD 2026-05-13T23:00Z; Marc Broussard, 'Thu May 14', 'Thursday, May 14, 2026, Show 7:30 PM', 2026-05-14T23:30Z; Matchbox 30, 'Wed Sep 30', 'Wednesday, September 30, 2026', 2026-09-30T23:30Z. In all 7 copies the card date equals the JSON-LD startDate in America/New_York on every card (0 mismatches; settle and second check). Caution for compiling: 34 of the 68 rows are 8 pm shows that the source writes as T00:00:00+00:00 on the next day (Southern Culture on the Skids: card 'Fri May 15', JSON-LD 2026-05-16T00:00Z). Amendment 12's midnight item reads such a value as date-only and would date them one day late. The settle `shows.csv` writes these starts as the same instant in local offset (e.g. 2026-05-15T20:00:00-04:00), which matches the card's 'Show: 8:00 PM'; the second check notes that this is not the start as the source writes it. No such row falls on 1 Apr, 1 Jul or 1 Oct, so S1 and S2 are the same either way. No start falls between 00:01 and 04:59 (Amendment 3).
- robots.txt (15 Oct 2026 UTC): allowed. https://ardmoremusichall.com/robots.txt (200, 234 bytes; the body was not saved, and the rules are as recorded in the check): 'User-agent: *' disallows only /wp-admin/, /drink-displays/, /special-events-display-upstairs/, one PDF, /upcoming-displays/ and /upstairs-drink-displays/; the home page and the other pages read are allowed. polite.py allowed every URL read, and web.archive.org's robots.txt allows the snapshot and CDX paths used. No bot check and no 403 from the venue host (every reply 200 or 404); the 'captcha' strings in the copies are Contact Form 7 reCAPTCHA styles and the only 'cloudflare' string is a cdnjs normalize.css import.
- Terms (15-16 Oct 2026 UTC; re-checked 18 Oct 2026 on the 7 saved copies): copyright-only. The only notice is the footer line '© 2026 Ardmore Music Hall', a bare notice (Amendment 14). The site has no terms or privacy page: none is in page-sitemap.xml or the footer links, and the six standard paths are 404. No text on the pages read limits copying, reuse or automated reading, and no copy carries 'all rights reserved', terms or privacy text. Checked: https://ardmoremusichall.com/ (footer); https://ardmoremusichall.com/page-sitemap.xml; https://ardmoremusichall.com/terms/ (404); https://ardmoremusichall.com/terms-of-use/ (404); https://ardmoremusichall.com/terms-of-service/ (404); https://ardmoremusichall.com/terms-and-conditions/ (404); https://ardmoremusichall.com/legal/ (404); https://ardmoremusichall.com/privacy-policy/ (404); https://ardmoremusichall.com/faqs/; https://ardmoremusichall.com/about/; https://ardmoremusichall.com/private-events/; https://ardmoremusichall.com/contact-us/
- Platform: Self-hosted WordPress (generator WordPress 6.9.x in the copies, 7.1.3 live; theme material-design-google) with the 'sungraphic-events' plugin, which feeds Tixr events into the cards and JSON-LD, as on MilkBoy Philadelphia. Not Fatsoma, Squarespace, Wix, Music Glue or Webflow, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer in any copy or live page. Tickets go through Tixr and the gift-card shop is on square.site; neither hosts the site, and neither is Live Nation or AEG.
- Operator: owned by a private owner, head booker and owner since 2014, with the partners of the sister restaurant The Ripplewood (named on https://ardmoremusichall.com/about/, which says 'The club is independently owned, operated and booked.'). The venue names a person, not a company, as its booker. Operator for clustering (Amendment 1, owner or the company that books the shows): Rising Sun Presents. Its about page (https://risingsunpresents.com/about) says 'Ardmore Music Hall has been our flagship venue since 2014, managed by a core leadership team', that the company 'grew out of ongoing collaborations between the teams at Ardmore Music Hall, Point Entertainment, and Bonfire Entertainment', and that it provides 'services and management support for Underground Arts and MilkBoy (Philadelphia, PA)'; The 8x10's FAQ (https://the8x10.com/revival/) calls Ardmore Music Hall Rising Sun Presents' flagship venue; Ardmore's own FAQ (https://ardmoremusichall.com/faqs/) sells tickets for MilkBoy and 'other Rising Sun Presents Shows'. Rising Sun Presents is also MilkBoy Philadelphia's house booker, so Ardmore Music Hall joins the `rising-sun-presents` cluster (Amendment 1). No public source names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake or booker for the venue or for Rising Sun Presents (web searches in the check; see MilkBoy Philadelphia for Point Entertainment and Bonfire). Underground Arts, which the same about page lists among the rooms it supports, is not used on §1 (`candidates.md`: public sources name AEG Live (2015) and The Bowery Presents for it); that names no AEG stake in Rising Sun Presents, and Underground Arts is not in `venues.csv`, so it joins no cluster here. The 2013 reopening involved Point Entertainment and Bonfire Booking (https://news.pollstar.com/2013/07/23/philly-venue-reborn-2/, search snippet); the only Live Nation link found is one co-promoted show at another venue (The Mann), not a stake or a booking deal. Outside presenters on counted rows (named in the card's presented-by line): WXPN 7 of 64 (2 S1, 5 S2; one S1 row with Philadelphia Folksong Society), The Rock & Roll Playhouse 3 (S1), and one each for Le Poisson Rouge (with Rising Sun Presents, 14 Jun), Class of '91, Main Line Health and Vinyl Closet Records. Rising Sun Presents is named on that 1 row only. None is Live Nation or AEG and none presents more than half of the counted rows. One S1 row (20 May) names performers, one of them a private person, in that line, and 3 S2 rows (1 Aug, 9 Aug, 26 Sep) name an occasion there (a benefit, an afterparty, a celebration); none names a company, and they are left out of `presented_by`.
- Rooms: one. The visible venue-name field and the JSON-LD location are 'Ardmore Music Hall' on all 401 cards of the 7 copies, and there is no room filter. The cards also carry a hidden Tixr attribute, data-venue-id (7033 on 366 cards, 7040 on 35: kids' shows, showcases, private-event closures, brunch); it names no room and shows the same venue name, so it is not a room field (Amendment 14). Counted on 7033 alone the room would still have S1 22, S2 32. The balcony and upstairs spaces are offered only for private rentals and are not listed as rooms. Capacity 550: 'FULL VENUE BUYOUTS (Up to 550 Guests)' (https://ardmoremusichall.com/private-events/), the largest figure the venue publishes (Amendment 8); no concert capacity is published.
- Coverage: S1 >=27, S2 >=37. Settled on 17-18 Oct 2026 from saved copies, after web.archive.org stopped the 16-17 Oct check: the check had only the 12 May copy (S1 >=26, S2 >=19) and left the venue undecided. The seven copies were unioned on the Tixr numeric event id, the latest copy listing a show giving its fields: 139 ticket URLs make 127 events, 11 of them retitled under a new slug on the same date (7 in S1/S2). Keyed by URL alone the union would count retitles twice (28 / 44). 68 rows fall in 1 Apr - 30 Sep (S1 28, S2 40; 13 May to 30 Sep, all at Ardmore Music Hall); 4 'Closed For Private Event' rows are removed by the Amendment 4 words private event and closed (16 May in S1; 1 Jul, 11 Jul and 2 Aug in S2). No row is marked canceled or postponed, none is off-site and none is untitled. One S1 row (5 Jun, 'So Central Rain: REM Tribute + The Warhawks') is listed on 12 May and gone from the 5 Jun copy without a cancellation mark; it is counted, and S1 is >=26 without it. A second check re-parsed all 7 copies with its own script and got the same 68 rows and 27 / 37. These are lower bounds: no copy covers 1 Apr - 12 May (the scouting CDX file lists copies of 31 Mar, 14, 19, 22 and 23 Apr and 4, 6, 7 and 8 May that were not read), and shows announced after one copy and held before the next are missed; unread copies of 3 Jul, 7 Jul (03:29), 28 Jul (05:41) and 21 Aug could only raise the counts.
- Also checked: Ardmore (Lower Merion Township, Montgomery County, PA) is in the Philadelphia-Camden-Wilmington MSA (`candidates.md`, reading 6). It shares the `rising-sun-presents` cluster with MilkBoy Philadelphia, so neither passes §5 through the other; both pass through PhilaMOCA (`phl-philamoca`), and Philadelphia still has two clusters. Not in the Minneapolis-St Paul metro, and neither Hideout nor Black Cat (§6, Amendment 11).
- Requests during the eligibility check: ardmoremusichall.com 32 (14 on 15 Oct 2026 UTC, robots.txt included; 18 in the check of 16 Oct, which the record counts), ardmoremusic.com 3, web.archive.org 9 (15 Oct: 1 CDX query and the 12 May copy; 17 Oct 21:28-21:31 UTC: 1 CDX query and 6 copies, 30 s apart), risingsunpresents.com 2 and www.risingsunpresents.com 1 (17 Oct 23:19 UTC: robots.txt on each host and the about page). Source: requests.log.

### Aladdin Theater (`pdx-aladdin`, Portland)
- Source: The venue's own WordPress site (WLCR events plugin). Its home page lists every upcoming show (about 7-13 months ahead) as schema.org Event blocks keyed by data-event-id, with startDate, title, a separate event-venue field, an event-presented-by field, a doors/show line and the ticket status; the same rows come from the site's own feed https://www.aladdin-theater.com/wp-json/wlcr/v1/events/raw. The live feed and pages hold only upcoming shows (from 2026-10-16): no past events, empty WordPress posts, and a search endpoint that returns only upcoming events. So S1 and S2 come from web.archive.org copies of the own home page. The feed endpoint has 0 captures in the archive index.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://www.aladdin-theater.com/ for ts 20260301175626, 20260315133316, 20260401134533, 20260415134157, 20260501133621, 20260515133422, 20260523201003, 20260612230535, 20260707044945, 20260717214600, 20260812213937, 20260831160729 (12 copies, all used). The two CDX queries (aladdin-theater.com/ and www.aladdin-theater.com/, 200 rows each, 2026-03-01..2026-10-13) list 177 captures in Mar-Sep, dense to 2026-05-19, then only 05-23, 06-12, 07-06, 07-07, 07-17, 08-12 and three on 08-31; the unpicked 07-06 and 08-31 captures sit next to picked ones. Live (scouting, 16 Oct 2026): the home page, the events/raw feed, one search query and /wp-json/wp/v2/posts, for the source and date checks only; no live row is counted.
- Dates checked: The listed dates are show dates. The startDate meta writes local wall time with a false '+00:00' offset, and the data-event-doors attribute gives the true UTC doors time. Eliza McLamb - Good Story Tour (1 Apr copy): startDate '2026-04-01T20:00:00+00:00', 'Tonight', data-event-doors '2026-04-02T02:00:00Z' (7 pm PDT on 1 Apr), 'Doors: 7PM / Show: 8PM'. The Robert Cray Band (17 Jul copy): startDate '2026-08-07T20:00:00+00:00', 'Fri, August 7th, 2026', data-event-doors '2026-08-08T02:00:00Z', 'Doors: 7PM / Show: 8PM'. Live feed (16 Oct): Hovvdy 2026-10-16T20:00 = page 'Doors: 7PM / Show: 8PM'; The Okee Dokee Brothers 2026-10-18T11:00 = page 'Show: 11AM'. `shows.csv` writes the local time with the real America/Los_Angeles offset (-07:00). Starts in the periods run from 18:00 to 21:30 (counted rows from 18:30), so the 00:01-04:59 rule (Amendment 3) never applies, and reading the offset as UTC would not change any date.
- robots.txt (15-16 Oct 2026): allowed. polite.py allowed /, /wp-json/ and /wp-json/wlcr/v1/events/raw; Crawl-delay 10 s, honoured. aladdin-theater.com redirects to www.aladdin-theater.com. Every venue request answered 200 or 404, with no 403 and no bot check. web.archive.org allowed the CDX and snapshot paths used.
- Terms (16 Oct 2026): copyright-only. The only notice is the footer '© 2026 Aladdin. Website by WLCR' (wlcr.io), a bare notice (Amendment 14), in every saved body, the 12 archive copies included; there are no links to terms, privacy or legal pages, and no text on the site forbids copying or reuse or limits use to personal viewing. Checked: https://www.aladdin-theater.com/ (footer); https://www.aladdin-theater.com/terms/ (404); https://www.aladdin-theater.com/terms-of-use/ (404); https://www.aladdin-theater.com/terms-of-service/ (404); https://www.aladdin-theater.com/terms-and-conditions/ (404); https://www.aladdin-theater.com/legal/ (404); https://www.aladdin-theater.com/privacy-policy/ (404); https://www.aladdin-theater.com/ticket-information/; https://www.aladdin-theater.com/information/; https://www.aladdin-theater.com/about-us/; https://www.aladdin-theater.com/faq/
- Platform: WordPress on WP Engine, custom theme 'aladdin-wlcr' built by WLCR. Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Weebly, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer: 'ROCKHOUSE' occurs in none of the saved bodies, and there is no RHP plugin. Etix is used only for ticket links on the event blocks and does not host the site. No Cloudflare challenge, Turnstile or captcha marker; all 12 archive copies are full listings (title 'Aladdin', 74 to 98 list-style events each, WLCR footer).
- Operator (§1, Amendment 14): owner True West LLC, a Portland promoter. The about page says the theater 'is owned today by True West' and 'True West produces concerts at the Aladdin Theater, Revolution Hall, the Oregon Zoo Summer Concerts Series ...' (https://www.aladdin-theater.com/about-us/); the information page names its current owner, a private owner 'of True West LLC' (https://www.aladdin-theater.com/information/). House booker: True West (True West Presents), named by the venue as producing its concerts and presenting 75 of the 89 counted rows (84%; S1 49 of 58, S2 26 of 31; labels 'True West Presents', 'TRUE WEST PRESENTS', 'True West and T Present'). Owner and house booker are the same company, so the operator is True West LLC. Every counted row names a presenter. Outside presenters on counted rows: Live Nation ('LIVE NATION PRESENTS') 2 (both S1: 2 May and 17 May), KMHD 2 (1 + 1), J-Fell Presents 2 (S1), and Science On Tap, Step Forward Films, SPG, Field Guide Events, Soul'd Out & Pickathon and Fruit Wine Productions 1 each; 2 S1 rows say 'Aladdin Theater Presents' (the house). None of them presents more than half, so none is a house booker and none joins a cluster. No public source found names a Live Nation or AEG owner, stake, house booker or booking deal for True West or the Aladdin (web searches of the scouting check and of its skeptic). A 2024 Business Wire release calls True West 'one of the leading independent concert promoters and event producers in the Pacific Northwest' (https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240220137068/en/, search snippet); a February 2026 PDX Live press release (thesquarepdx.org) still calls it an independent promoter; a 2024 Portland Monthly article ties AEG's Portland partnership to Monqui, not True West. The FAQ (https://www.aladdin-theater.com/faq/) names the house box office and Etix ticketing. The skeptic corrected the settle report's 'No AEG presenter': it holds for counted rows only, since the 1 Mar copy has 'AEG & True West Present' on a 20 Mar 2026 show, outside S1 and S2; this does not change the reading. Under the literal reading of Amendment 1 (Amendment 14, last item), Live Nation is an operator of this room through its 2 counted S1 rows, so the room would fail §1 in that report, as Rio Theatre would.
- Rooms: one. The separate event-venue field has one on-site value, 'Aladdin Theater'. Its other values are other buildings ('- Crystal Ballroom -', '- McMenamins Grand Lodge -', '- Moore Theatre -', '- Roseland Theater -', '- Keller Auditorium -', 'Arlene Schnitzer Concert Hall'), and those rows are `off-site` (Amendment 1). Capacity 600 ('The Aladdin Theater is a 600 capacity venue', https://www.aladdin-theater.com/information/; the FAQ says it 'seats about 600 people').
- Coverage: S1 >=58, S2 >=31, both lower bounds. Settled on the night of 17-18 Oct 2026 from saved copies (fetched 18 Oct 00:50-01:03 UTC and read offline in settle batch 4), after web.archive.org's second 429 stopped the 16-17 Oct check, when the scouting record could not count S1 or S2. The count is the union of the 12 copies keyed by WLCR event id (latest copy wins): 105 rows in the periods, of which 3 are canceled or postponed (1 S1, 2 S2; marked only by title prefixes such as 'POSTPONED TBD:' and 'RESCHEDULED ... & MOVED TO POLARIS HALL:') and 13 are `off-site` (3 S1, 10 S2: Crystal Ballroom 4, McMenamins Grand Lodge 3, Arlene Schnitzer Concert Hall 2, Moore Theatre 2, Roseland Theater 1, Keller Auditorium 1). Amendment 4 words remove 0 rows; no title is empty. By month: Apr 22, May 22, Jun 14, Jul 4, Aug 9, Sep 18. An independent skeptic re-parsed the 12 saved bodies with its own script and got the same 105 rows, flags and counts (58 / 31), and found 8 sampled rows in their copies. Its notes: two counted rows vanished from the listing before their date without a cancellation mark (2 May, a Live Nation row, last listed 15 Mar; 2 Aug, last listed 7 Jul), and without them S1 is 57 and S2 30; three nights have early and late sets as two rows (two in S1, one in S2), 56 / 30 counted once, 55 / 29 with both changes. Every variant stays >= 20. No date in Apr-Sep lies outside every copy's listing window, but a show announced and played between two copies is missed: the longest spans are 23 May-11 Jun (20 d), 12 Jun-6 Jul (25 d), 17 Jul-11 Aug (26 d) and 12-30 Aug (19 d), and nothing announced after 31 Aug is seen. Only 2 of the 105 rows were first seen less than 21 days before their date. July is thin: the 7 Jul copy lists nothing from 7 to 16 Jul. Programme: mostly music nights, with comedy, talks and podcasts and touring film nights that pass the Amendment 4 words and count (Notes for compiling, scope).
- §5: no other eligible Portland venue of another cluster is in `venues.csv` yet. Cluster `true-west`: True West owns the Aladdin and is its house booker. Three other Portland candidates of `candidates.md` were settled on 18 Oct 2026 (settle batch 5) and meet §1-4: Revolution Hall (Revolution Hall 54 / 45, Show Bar 27 / 23), whose house booker is True West (69 of 99 counted main-hall rows), so it shares this cluster; Mississippi Studios ('Mississippi Studios Presents' on 127 of 135 counted rows); and Polaris Hall ('Polaris Hall Presents' on every counted row). Polaris Hall's footer links Mississippi Studios, Revolution Hall, TrueWest and Polaris Hall as one 'family of music venues', Revolution Hall's settle report gives its owners as Mississippi Studios' owners with True West, and 2014-15 press (search snippets) calls Revolution Hall a True West joint venture with Mississippi Studios, so all four may be one cluster; Portland passes §5 either way through Wonder Ballroom, settled eligible on 18 Oct 2026 as a cluster of its own (`monqui-presents`; see its section below). Two canceled Aladdin rows say they moved to Polaris Hall under 'True West Presents'; Faetooth (11 May), which Polaris Hall's listing marks 'MOVED TO ALADDIN THEATER' and drops as off-site, is counted here once. §5 is left to the lead. §6: Portland is not in the Minneapolis-St Paul metro, and the venue is neither Hideout nor Black Cat (Amendment 11).
- Requests during the eligibility check: aladdin-theater.com 2 (robots.txt and the redirect, 15 Oct), www.aladdin-theater.com 18 (3 on 15 Oct with robots.txt, 15 on 16 Oct; 11 answered 200, 7 404; the record counts 15), web.archive.org 27 on 18 Oct (3 CDX queries, one of them for the feed endpoint, and 24 copy requests: both home-page CDX queries picked the same 12 captures, so each was requested twice and saved once). The settle and its skeptic sent no request. Source: requests.log.

### Mississippi Studios (`pdx-mississippi`, Portland)
- Source: The venue's own WordPress site. Its home page carries two tabs drawn server-side from Etix: a full list of the next 30 events of both buildings the site sells (event__item blocks with a data-venue-id field and venue logo, an h5 date, a presented-by field, the title linking its Etix ticket, a data-event-doors UTC time and a 'Doors: / Show:' line), paged at /full-view/N and repeated at /compact-view/N, and a calendar tab, a month grid of every announced upcoming event with its own event-venue field and date-only day headers (153 to 196 events per copy; the 16 Mar 2026 copy runs to 4 Dec). The live site holds only upcoming shows (from 16 Oct 2026): the calendar's previous-month button is disabled and /calendar/?month=202607 returns months 202610 to 202706, there are no own event pages, no event post type in /wp-json/wp/v2/types and no event sitemap. So S1 and S2 come from web.archive.org copies of the own home, /full-view/N, /compact-view/N and /calendar/ pages. truewestpresents.com, which lists the venue under its Venues menu, holds only upcoming shows too and is not used. Settled eligible from saved copies (see Coverage); the 16-17 Oct record had it undecided.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://mississippistudios.com/ for ts 20260303051842, 20260310133426, 20260316232051, 20260413182513, 20260423033846, 20260514232810, 20260531072421, 20260615150525, 20260712032535, 20260801040408, 20260802234449, 20260919093013 (12 of the 28 captures in the CDX list); /full-view/2 for ts 20260413180345 and 20260423033856; /full-view/3 for ts 20260413180639, 20260423033902 and 20260717212609; /compact-view/2 for ts 20260413180832 and 20260727003418; https://www.mississippistudios.com/calendar/ for ts 20260518100732 (20 copies, all used, all status 200 and all real listings). The seven CDX queries (2026-03-01..2026-10-15, status 200) were mississippistudios.com/ (28 rows, 12 picked), /full-view/ (0), /full-view/2/ (2, both picked), /full-view/3/ (3, all picked), /compact-view/ (0), /compact-view/2/ (3, 2 picked) and /calendar/ (1, picked); the one unpicked home capture before mid-April, 20260410155406, is not needed (see Coverage). - Dates checked: The listed dates are show dates. Live list (16 Oct 2026): 'Friday, October 16, 2026', 'Doors: 7PM', data-event-doors '2026-10-17T02:00:00Z' (7 pm PDT on 16 Oct). 13 Apr copy: Throwing Muses, 'Thursday, April 16, 2026', data-event-doors '2026-04-17T03:00:00Z' (8 pm PDT on 16 Apr), 'Doors: 8PM / Show: 9 PM'; `shows.csv` writes 2026-04-16T21:00:00-07:00. 16 Mar copy: Tokyo Tea Room, 'Wednesday, April 1, 2026', 'Doors: 7PM / Show: 8 PM', written 2026-04-01T20:00:00-07:00. The settle takes the show time at or after the doors time, in America/Los_Angeles with its real offset; counted full-list starts run from 19:30 to 22:00 (the one 16:00 row is the bingo row, removed), so the 00:01-04:59 rule (Amendment 3) never applies. Calendar rows give only the day header, so their listed date is used (Amendment 3, date-only value); that is how the 14 calendar-only counted rows are dated (e.g. Westerman, 6 Apr, from the 3, 10 and 16 Mar copies).
- robots.txt (15-16 Oct 2026): allowed. https://mississippistudios.com/robots.txt is a Yoast block, 'User-agent: *' with an empty 'Disallow:', and a stray 'Crawl-delay: 10' before any group; polite.py allowed every path read (delay 2.5 s). truewestpresents.com has the same file and was allowed; shop.mississippistudios.com's robots.txt allowed its terms page. Every venue request answered 200 or 404, with no 403 and no bot check. web.archive.org allowed the CDX and snapshot paths used; the copies were fetched on 18 Oct 2026 by the prefetch through polite.py (30 s gap).
- Terms (16 Oct 2026; re-checked on the 20 saved copies): none-found for the listings. The only notice on the site is the footer '© 2025 Mississippi Studios', a bare notice (Amendment 14), in every saved body; none of the 20 copies contains 'reserved' or 'reproduce', and no text on the pages read forbids copying, reproducing, republishing or automated reading, or limits use to personal viewing. The privacy policy is a Shopify-style template that mentions 'our website's Terms of Service', and the site has no such page. The only such terms are the venue's merch shop's, https://shop.mississippistudios.com/policies/terms-of-service (Shopify), which ban use of the site 'to spam, phish, pharm, pretext, spider, crawl, or scrape' and say 'You agree not to reproduce, duplicate, copy, sell, resell or exploit any portion of the Service'. They open with 'This website is operated by Mississippi Studios Merch Store', and the privacy-policy sentence that points to them is about 'our store's website' ('Once you leave our store's website ... you are no longer governed by this Privacy Policy or our website's Terms of Service'), so they are read as covering only the shop, not the mississippistudios.com listings, as the scouting check's skeptic judged; if they were read as reaching the main site, or as unclear, §3 would fail and the venue would be out in both readings. Checked: https://mississippistudios.com/ (footer); https://mississippistudios.com/terms/ (404); https://mississippistudios.com/terms-of-use/ (404); https://mississippistudios.com/terms-of-service/ (404); https://mississippistudios.com/terms-and-conditions/ (404); https://mississippistudios.com/legal/ (404); https://mississippistudios.com/privacy-policy/; https://mississippistudios.com/page-sitemap.xml (no terms page listed); https://shop.mississippistudios.com/policies/terms-of-service
- Platform: WordPress with the custom theme 'mississippi-studios-wlcr' ('Website by WLCR.io'); the only plugin seen is gravityforms. Rows come from Etix and are rendered on the venue's own pages; Etix is used only for ticket links and does not host the site. Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Weebly, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer: 'ROCKHOUSE' occurs in none of the 20 copies, and there is no RHP plugin. Shopify hosts only the merch shop. No Cloudflare challenge, captcha or 403: all 20 copies are the venue's own home template with 30 full-list rows, the calendar tab and the WLCR footer.
- Operator (§1, Amendment 14): owner the Mississippi Studios ownership group, privately held, whose co-owners are private owners named in press; the venue info page says 'Built, owned and operated by and for musicians.', and the site's privacy policy (also served on polarishall.com) says 'We operate Mississippi Studios, Bar Bar and Polaris Hall.'. The record cites Wikipedia for Mississippi Productions Inc. as the parent of Good George LLC, Revolution Hall's owner; no saved page names the company that owns Mississippi Studios itself, so that company is recorded as not identified. House booker: the venue itself, named by the venue ('booking requests & artist information: booking@mississippistudios.com', https://mississippistudios.com/venue-info-mississippi-studios/) and presenting most counted rows: 'Mississippi Studios Presents' on 127 of the 148 counted rows (86%); the 14 calendar-only rows carry no presenter field, and on full-list counted rows it is 127 of 134 (95%). Owner and house booker are the same group, so the operator is Mississippi Studios. Outside presenters on counted rows: Stump Factory 3 (S1, 'STUMPFEST XI', 23-25 Apr), Days Of Bowie 1, Low Bar Chorale 1, Willamette Week 1 and J-Fell 1 (co-presenting with the house, 'Mississippi Studios & J-Fell Present', 18 Jul), each under half, so none is a house booker and none joins a cluster. The settle's and the skeptic's one 'True West Presents' row is 'MOVED TO ALADDIN THEATER: Deeper Sleeper' (19 May), which is off-site (see Coverage), so True West presents no counted row here. No Live Nation or AEG presenter on any row, and none of the 20 copies contains Live Nation, AEG, Ticketmaster or AXS; ticketing is Etix, and searches of the scouting check and its skeptic found no Live Nation or AEG owner, stake, house booker or booking deal at Mississippi Studios or True West. Under the literal reading of Amendment 1 (Amendment 14, last item), Stump Factory, Days Of Bowie, Low Bar Chorale, Willamette Week and J-Fell are also operators of this room; J-Fell presents at the Aladdin Theater (cluster `true-west`), so that report joins the two clusters.
- Rooms: one. The venue field (data-venue-id 16896 'Mississippi Studios' and 16900 'Polaris Hall' with their logos in the full list, the event-venue div in the calendar) holds only buildings: Mississippi Studios, Polaris Hall (another building; its rows are `off-site`) and, in the calendar only, First Congregational United Church of Christ (`off-site`). No Etix id is shown under two venues. Within Mississippi Studios there is one music room; Bar Bar is the bar and restaurant with no show field, and 'on Bar Bar Patio' occurs only in one title (title text, not a room, Amendment 1). The settle wrote the rows as `mississippistudios-com:house`; that is the whole venue, named after its building, not a split venue's pool room, so it is the venue's one room and a test room (as Martyrs', Hotel Utah and Polaris Hall).
- Coverage: S1 >=81, S2 >=67 for the one room (lower bounds; by month Apr 28, May 27, Jun 26; Jul 17, Aug 24, Sep 26). Settled on 18 Oct 2026 from saved copies (fetched 18 Oct 02:55-03:08 UTC and read offline in settle batch 5) after web.archive.org's second 429 stopped the 16-17 Oct check, when the scouting record could not count S1 or S2. The settle counted 72 / 63 from the full-list tab only (union by Etix id, else listed date and title, the latest copy winning; 1 row removed by word, 0 canceled, 0 untitled, off-site 52 / 65). An independent skeptic re-parsed the 20 saved bodies with its own parser and got the same full-list result, but found that the settle missed the calendar tab in all 20 copies: with it, 14 more counted rows (S1 10, 2-12 Apr; S2 4: 10 Jul, 12 Aug, 15 Aug, 24 Sep) and 10 more off-site rows (off-site 60 / 67, Polaris Hall plus the church), for S1 82, S2 67. The skeptic's recount wins over the settle's. One of its 82 S1 rows, 'MOVED TO ALADDIN THEATER: Deeper Sleeper' (19 May, venue field Mississippi Studios, 'True West Presents', listed so in the 14 and 18 May copies), is held at another building and is `off-site` under Amendment 1, as Polaris Hall's 'MOVED TO ALADDIN THEATER: Faetooth' row (11 May) is in both listings and as Amager Bio's 'Flyttet til Loppen' row was in its settle; the Aladdin Theater's count already holds Deeper Sleeper on 19 May. So S1 is 81 here and off-site 61 / 67; neither the settle nor the skeptic dropped it. Amendment 4 words remove 1 row, 'Plants, Drag & Bingo: YES PLEASE on Bar Bar Patio' (4 Jun, bingo), whose `drop_reason` the settle left empty in `shows.csv` (a count on that file gives S1 73 on the full list); it is not counted. 0 canceled, 0 untitled. Six counted rows vanished from later copies that cover their dates without a cancel mark (S1: Half Shadow, 5 Apr, last listed 10 Mar, and Smoking Popes Night 1 and 2, 16-17 Jun, last listed 23 Apr; S2: 12 Aug, 15 Aug and 24 Sep); without them S1 is 78 and S2 64 (the skeptic's conservative 79 / 64, less Deeper Sleeper). With the calendar tab no night in S1 or S2 lies outside every copy's listing (the 16 Mar copy's calendar covers 2-12 Apr, which no full-list copy does). A show announced and played between two copies is missed: the longest spans without a copy are 16 Mar-13 Apr, 15 Jun-12 Jul and 2 Aug-19 Sep. Every variant stays far above 20. Programme: mostly music nights (touring and local bands, singer-songwriters, tribute singalongs), with a few comedy nights.
- Capacity: no figure published by the venue; the venue info page gives only 'events ranging in size from 20 - 700 guests' across its properties for rentals. The 249 of Songkick and SeatPick is not used (Amendment 8).
- §5: Portland (Portland-Vancouver-Hillsboro, OR-WA MSA, OMB Bulletin 23-01). Cluster `pdx-mississippi`, with Polaris Hall: the saved privacy policy of both sites says 'We operate Mississippi Studios, Bar Bar and Polaris Hall.', Polaris Hall's FAQ sends bands to 'booking@mississippistudios.com', and Mississippi Studios' own listing carries Polaris Hall's rows ('Polaris Hall Presents'), so the two share their operator and booking office. A shared owner or house booker with True West (cluster `true-west`: the Aladdin Theater, and Revolution Hall, whose house booker is True West) is not shown by the saved bodies: the menu 'Visit our partners revolution hall polaris hall true west' and the footer 'VISIT OUR FAMILY OF MUSIC', linking revolutionhall.com, polarishall.com, mississippistudios.com and truewestpresents.com, in all 20 copies, and True West's Venues menu listing Mississippi Studios and Polaris Hall, name partners, not an owner or a booker; True West's about page and the Aladdin's about page name the concerts it produces at the Aladdin Theater and Revolution Hall, not at Mississippi Studios; and True West presents no counted row here. Revolution Hall's saved privacy policy gives info@mississippistudios.com and 3939 N. Mississippi Ave. (this venue's address) as its contact, and its scouting record gives its owner as the Mississippi Studios owners with True West (press, not a saved body); if that shared owner is taken as shown, Revolution Hall joins both clusters and Mississippi Studios, Polaris Hall, Revolution Hall and the Aladdin Theater are one cluster, and Portland then passes §5 through Wonder Ballroom, settled eligible on 18 Oct 2026 as a cluster of its own (see its section below), as it does if they are not. Kept as its own cluster here; §5 is left to the lead. §6: Portland is not in the Minneapolis-St Paul metro, and the venue is neither Hideout nor Black Cat (Amendment 11).
- Requests during the eligibility check: mississippistudios.com 19 (requests.log: 3 on 15 Oct 2026 UTC, 18:49, robots.txt, the home page and /terms; 14 on 16 Oct UTC, 22:34-22:37, robots.txt, the home page, two sitemaps, /wp-json/wp/v2/types, the privacy policy, the venue info page, /full-view/?month=7, /calendar/?month=202607 and five terms guesses; 2 in the scouting check's skeptic, 22:38, the home page and the privacy policy; 13 answered 200, 6 404), shop.mississippistudios.com 2 (the skeptic, 22:39, robots.txt and the terms page), truewestpresents.com 7 (16 Oct, 22:36, robots.txt and the home page of www.truewestpresents.com, and of truewestpresents.com robots.txt twice, the home, about and sitemap pages; the record's figures are 15 and 5), web.archive.org 27 on 18 Oct 2026 UTC, 02:55-03:08 (7 CDX queries and the 20 copies). Nine later requests to truewestpresents.com on 16 Oct (22:40-22:41 and 23:04-23:05 UTC) fall in other Portland venues' checks and are not counted here. The settle and its skeptic sent no request. Source: requests.log.
- Note for compiling: compile must read the calendar tab of every copy as well as the full list; calendar rows give only the date and the event-venue, no time and no presenter, so a show seen in both keeps the full list's start and presenter. The compact tab repeats the full-list blocks without data-venue-id and must not be counted twice. `shows.csv` must add the 14 calendar-only counted rows and the calendar-only off-site rows, write `non-music` on the bingo row and `off-site` on the Deeper Sleeper row. Five rows lose their Etix link near their date and were merged by date and title with the earlier linked row.

### Polaris Hall (`pdx-polaris`, Portland)
- Source: The venue's own HTML listing on https://polarishall.com/ (a custom Craft CMS site with grid and calendar views). Each show is an event card (<div class="event...">) with a date line ('Wednesday, Apr 1'), a presenter field ('Polaris Hall Presents'), the title, a support line, doors and show times, stickers ('All Ages', 'Sold Out') and an Etix ticket link. The live site lists only coming shows (from 2026-10-16): the calendar view with a month parameter returns the same month keys (2026-10 to 2027-05), /past-shows and /sitemap.xml are 404, and ?search is filtered in the browser, so the server returns the same list. The operator site truewestpresents.com (WordPress) has no event type in its REST types, and its calendar holds coming shows only. So S1 and S2 come from web.archive.org copies of the own home page. The 16-17 Oct record could not count them, because polite.py refused web.archive.org after the 16 Oct stop.
- Read: https://web.archive.org/web/<ts>id_/https://polarishall.com/ for ts 20260317001310, 20260412122150, 20260510225646, 20260520010938, 20260613092153, 20260707055757, 20260727003540 (7 copies, all used; 2 stored gzip-compressed); the CDX list of polarishall.com/ (1 Mar to 15 Oct 2026, status 200), 10 captures, of which 20260610035252, 20260707013217 and 20260717212608 were not picked; live (scouting, 15-16 Oct 2026 UTC): the home page, ?view=calendar, ?view=calendar&month=2026-07, ?search=the, /privacy-policy, /information and /rentals, and truewestpresents.com /, /about-us/ and /wp-json/wp/v2/types, for the source, date, operator and terms checks only; no live row is counted. The skeptic of the record also read saved bodies of the Mississippi Studios check (mississippistudios.com/calendar/?q=&month=202607, truewestpresents.com/partners/, truewestpresents.com/?style=calendar&calendar_date=1775001600 and https://shop.mississippistudios.com/policies/terms-of-service).
- Dates checked: These are show dates. 17 Mar copy: 'Wednesday, Apr 1 Polaris Hall Presents Tara Clerkin Trio ... Doors 7PM Show 8PM'. 27 Jul copy: 'Wednesday, Sep 23 Sold Out ... Polaris Hall Presents Solya ... Doors 7PM Show 8PM'. The year is not printed; it is taken from the capture date and checked against the weekday, with 0 mismatches in the 569 cards of the 7 copies (the skeptic's count). The live list confirms the rule: its earliest row is 'Friday, Oct 16' (2026), and the rows dated Apr 8, Apr 20 and May 17 fall on a Thursday, Tuesday and Monday, which places them in 2027. Start is the show time, or the doors time if no show time is given, local time. Counted starts run from 09:30 (8 Aug, a morning sound-healing session) to 21:30, so the 00:01-04:59 rule (Amendment 3) moves no date.
- robots.txt (15 and 16 Oct 2026 UTC): allowed. https://polarishall.com/robots.txt and https://www.polarishall.com/robots.txt are 404, so all paths are allowed; www.polarishall.com redirects to polarishall.com. polite.py allowed truewestpresents.com. web.archive.org allowed the CDX and snapshot paths used by the prefetch on 18 Oct.
- Terms (16 Oct 2026 UTC; the 7 copies read 18 Oct): none-found. The footer reads only '©2026 Polaris Hall', a bare notice (Amendment 14), on the live site and in all 7 copies. The privacy policy is the operator's template ('Our website address is: mississippistudios.com. We operate Mississippi Studios, Bar Bar and Polaris Hall.') and mentions 'our website's Terms of Service', but the site links no terms, and the guessed terms pages are 404. The only such terms that exist are the Shopify template of the operator's merch store (https://shop.mississippistudios.com/policies/terms-of-service), which bans copying 'any portion of the Service' and use 'to spam, phish, pharm, pretext, spider, crawl, or scrape'; their scope is 'This website is operated by Mississippi Studios Merch Store', so they are read as not reaching polarishall.com, as in the record and in Mississippi Studios' own check. A stricter reviewer could call §3 unclear here, and the same would then hold for Mississippi Studios. The Google reCAPTCHA script serves the forms only. Checked: https://polarishall.com/ (footer); https://polarishall.com/privacy-policy; https://polarishall.com/information; https://polarishall.com/rentals; https://polarishall.com/terms (404); https://polarishall.com/terms-of-service (404); https://polarishall.com/terms-of-use (404); https://polarishall.com/terms-and-conditions (404); https://polarishall.com/legal (404); https://truewestpresents.com/ ('© 2026 TrueWest. Website by WLCR.io', no terms link); https://truewestpresents.com/about-us/
- Platform: custom Craft CMS (the live pages carry Craft's 'CRAFT_CSRF_TOKEN' meta tag); tickets are sold through Etix, which appears only as ticket links and poster images and does not host the site. Not Squarespace, Wix, Fatsoma, Music Glue or Webflow, and no 'Powered by ROCKHOUSE PARTNERS an ETIX company' footer: 'ROCKHOUSE' and 'Powered by' occur in none of the 7 copies or the live home page. No bot check and no 403 in front of the listings: every venue-host request was a 200 or a 404, and all 7 copies are full listings (title 'Polaris Hall', 70, 70, 72, 81, 91, 91 and 94 event cards).
- Operator (§1, Amendment 14): owner: the press found in scouting (Portland Mercury, 28 Jul 2017, https://www.portlandmercury.com/music/2017/07/28/19196631/the-owners-of-mississippi-studios-just-bought-the-north-star-ballroom, and Willamette Week, 27 Jul 2017, https://www.wweek.com/music/2017/07/27/mississippi-studios-is-turning-a-north-portland-events-space-into-a-part-time-music-venue/; search results, not saved) says the owners of Mississippi Studios, private owners, bought the North Star Ballroom and made it Polaris Hall; no owning company is named in the saved bodies. House booker: Mississippi Studios. The venue names it: 'My band wants to play at Polaris Hall, who do I contact? Email booking@mississippistudios.com', with submissions to 'Mississippi Studios - Attn: Booking' (https://polarishall.com/information), and the privacy policy of both sites says 'We operate Mississippi Studios, Bar Bar and Polaris Hall.' (https://polarishall.com/privacy-policy, https://mississippistudios.com/privacy-policy/). Mississippi Studios' own listing also carries the Polaris Hall rows (its settle report drops them as off-site). The presenter field reads 'Polaris Hall Presents', the house label, on all 123 rows written to `shows.csv` (and on all 569 cards of the copies). Outside presenters named in titles: Low Bar Chorale 1 counted S1 row (3 Apr) and FunkyFrogBait 1 S2 row (3 Sep, removed by the word rule), each far under half, so neither is a house booker and neither joins a cluster. No saved body names Live Nation, AEG, Goldenvoice or Ticketmaster (the skeptic's search of all 7 copies), ticketing is Etix, and the record's web searches found no Live Nation or AEG owner, stake or booking deal; the record's own second check could not repeat the news search (search budget used up), so nothing from 2026 rules a stake out beyond the venue's pages. §1 passes. True West: the footer of every copy reads 'Visit our family of music venues' with links to Mississippi Studios, Revolution Hall, TrueWest and Polaris Hall, and truewestpresents.com lists Polaris Hall in its Venues menu and its calendar; but no saved body names True West as owner or booker of Polaris Hall, its about page lists its concerts at 'Aladdin Theater, Revolution Hall, Woodland Park Zoo, Pioneer Courthouse Square and many other venues' without Polaris Hall, the Polaris rows on its calendar read 'Polaris Hall Presents', and 'True West Presents' is on none of the 121 counted rows. The shared Etix kiosks (at Bar Bar and Show Bar, selling for Polaris Hall, Mississippi Studios, Revolution Hall, Show Bar and Aladdin Theater) are ticketing, not ownership. So Polaris Hall shares its operator with Mississippi Studios, while a direct tie to True West is not shown; it joins True West's cluster through its owners (§5 below). Under the literal reading of Amendment 1 (Amendment 14, last item), Low Bar Chorale is an operator of this room through its 1 counted row; 'Low Bar Chorale Presents' also appears among the presenters of the Revolution Hall and Mississippi Studios settle reports.
- Rooms: one. The cards have no room or venue field (only date, presenter, title, performers, times and the Etix link) and the page has no stage filter. The rentals page describes one concert hall with two bars and a lounge in the same building, rented as one ('Your rental of Polaris Hall is for the full building'). The settle wrote the rows as `polarishall-com:house`; that is the whole venue, not a split venue's pool room (Amendment 14's pool-room rule is for rows of a split venue), so it is the venue's one room and a test room, as for Martyrs' and Hotel Utah.
- Capacity: no figure published by the venue (/rentals gives only 'Over 4,500 square feet of space'; /information gives none).
- Coverage: S1 >=59, S2 >=62 for the one room (lower bounds; by month Apr 18, May 22, Jun 19; Jul 18, Aug 25, Sep 19), settled on 17-18 Oct 2026 from saved copies (fetched 18 Oct 2026, 03:25-03:28 UTC, and read offline in settle batch 5) after web.archive.org stopped the 16-17 Oct check, when the record could not count S1 or S2. The count is the union of the 7 copies keyed by Etix ticket URL, the latest copy that lists a show winning: 123 rows in the periods, of which 1 is `off-site` (S1, 11 May, 'MOVED TO ALADDIN THEATER: Faetooth'; Aladdin Theater counts it once) and 1 is removed by the Amendment 4 words (S2, 3 Sep, 'comedy'); 0 canceled, 0 untitled. An independent skeptic re-parsed the 7 saved bodies with its own script and got the same 123 (date, title) pairs and the same counts (59 / 62), and found 8 sampled rows in the copies named by `source_url`; it corrected nothing. Its notes: three counted rows vanished from a later copy before their date without a cancellation mark (25 Jun, gone by the 10 May copy; 17 Jul, gone by 7 Jul; 22 Sep, gone by 27 Jul), and without them S1 is 58 and S2 60; one Etix id was reused for a show moved from 26 Jun to 23 Sep, already in the 13 Jun copy, so it is one S2 row; another reused id (21 Mar, then 29 May) affects only a date outside the periods. Two S1 rows on 12 Apr are the early and late show of one stand-up act (58 counted once), and some counted titles look like non-music on their face (the 12 Apr stand-up shows, 'Cinema Therapy LIVE!' on 1 Aug); Amendment 4 is a whole-word rule, so they count, and coverage holds by a wide margin either way. Each copy lists 5 to 10 months ahead, so no date in Apr-Sep lies outside every copy's window; shows announced and played between two copies are missed (17 Mar-12 Apr, 12 Apr-10 May, 20 May-13 Jun and 13 Jun-7 Jul, 24-28 days each), and nothing added or canceled after the last capture (27 Jul) is seen. The three unpicked captures sit between picked ones and could only add rows.
- §5: Portland (Portland-Vancouver-Hillsboro, OR-WA MSA, OMB Bulletin 23-01) has no other eligible venue of another cluster in `venues.csv` yet. Cluster `true-west`. Polaris Hall shares its house booker and operator with Mississippi Studios (settled 18 Oct 2026, batch 5; the settle notes give house 72 / 63 and skeptic 82 / 67, and its section here counts S1 >=81, S2 >=67, with one 'MOVED TO ALADDIN THEATER' row off-site), and its owners are the owners of Mississippi Studios (the 2017 press above). The same press names Mississippi Studios' owners as owners of Revolution Hall with True West, and True West is the house booker of Revolution Hall and of the Aladdin Theater, both written in cluster `true-west`. Operator means the owner or the house booker, and either one joins rooms (Amendment 1), so Mississippi Studios and Polaris Hall go in that cluster too if the shared owner is taken as shown, as Revolution Hall's section says; the record and the Mississippi Studios record also put the three venues of the owner group in one cluster, and the venue's footer names all four as one 'family of music venues'. The owner ties rest on press and a Wikipedia snippet found by search (not saved); the saved bodies show the shared operator ('We operate Mississippi Studios, Bar Bar and Polaris Hall'; Revolution Hall's privacy policy gives the Mississippi Studios address and info@mississippistudios.com) but name no owning company. So Portland has one cluster besides Wonder Ballroom, which was settled eligible on 18 Oct 2026 as a cluster of its own (see its section below), and passes §5 through it. §5 is left to the lead. §6: Portland is not in the Minneapolis-St Paul metro, and the venue is neither Hideout nor Black Cat (Amendment 11).

### Revolution Hall (`pdx-revhall-*`, Portland)
- Source: The venue's own self-hosted WordPress site (WLCR events theme).
