## Operators, clusters and presenters (reading 1 of `candidates.md`)
Outside presenters' shares, where they were measured, are under half of a room's shows, except for the companies that present more
than half of a room's counted rows and are named as its operator in `venues.csv` (Amendment 14): Triple D's at The EARL, Rival
Entertainment at Center Stage, and Parallel, Paradiso and Podcastnetwerk in Tolhuistuin's Club, house room and Tuinhuis. No cluster
in `venues.csv` joins rooms of two buildings: MODO-LIVE owns The Pearl (`modo-live`) and also Lee's Palace in Toronto. Lee's Palace
and Dance Cave were settled eligible on 18 Oct 2026 in the `modo-live` cluster, but Toronto sorts after the cap, so they are not in
`venues.csv` (see "Eligible, after the cap") and the `modo-live` cluster there holds The Pearl alone.
- Literal reading of Amendment 1 (`candidates.md`): Paradiso presents a minority of Bitterzoet's shows, is credited with
  'i.s.m. paradiso' on 8 Cinetol rows and house-books Tolhuistuin's house room, which joins the three into one cluster; Loft Concerts
  joins Privatclub and Urban Spree; and counted rows presented by Live Nation (Rio Theatre, The Louisiana, Arts at the Armory) or by
  The Bowery Presents, which is AEG-affiliated (Arts at the Armory), drop those three on §1. Amsterdam keeps §5 through OCCII.
- Futuresound Group (owner and booker of The Wardrobe) presents 7 of 40 sampled Brudenell Social Club archive pages.
- Triple D's appears in 529's promoter list and presents 91 of The EARL's 102 compiled rows, so it is The EARL's house booker and
  its operator in `venues.csv` (Amendment 14). 529's compiled rows all name 529 as presenter, so the two stay separate clusters.
  Under the literal reading, Zero Mile (AEG) on 3 compiled rows drops The EARL on §1.
- MODO-LIVE presents some nights at Rickshaw Theatre and single shows at Horseshoe Tavern, whose largest outside presenter is an
  independent promoter on a minority of rows. No company presents more than half of either room's shows.
- The Castle Hotel hires its room to many promoters; the most frequent one named presents 4 of 54 counted rows. Badehaus's outside
  promoters present single shows. Festsaal Kreuzberg is run by an operating company of a promoters' association.
- 529's owner is not identified; no public source links the venue to Live Nation or AEG, so it passes §1 under reading 2.
- Under the literal reading of Amendment 1 (Amendment 14, last item), every company a show's own page names as presenting it goes into
  `presented_by` and is an operator for the sensitivity analysis reported beside the result.
- Make-Out Room was listed for sale in June 2026; no buyer was reported by mid-October. If a sale closes before the run, owner and booker
  are checked again.
- Presenter check of the eight buildings compiled on 18 Oct 2026 (the compile's presenter verdicts): of 74
  presenter names, 46 are not Live Nation or AEG, 24 were not identified, 3 are Live Nation and 1 is AEG. `ln-aeg.csv` (merge
  draft, `ln-aeg.csv`) keeps its 13 earlier rows and adds three, all live-nation: Shaky Knees
  (https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1335258/000133525826000009/lyv-20251231xex211subsidia.htm), c3
  (https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1335258/000133525826000009/lyv-20251231xex211subsidia.htm) and live nation
  (https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1335258/000133525826000009/lyv-20251231xex211subsidia.htm), Live Nation's FY2025
  10-K Exhibit 21.1, which lists Shaky Knees Fest, LLC and C3 Presents, L.L.C. as subsidiaries. Zero Mile was already there as
  aeg. Counted rows: Shaky Knees 1 (Center Stage Theater) and Zero Mile 1 (Vinyl) at Center Stage; live nation 2 and c3 1 at
  the Reggies Rock Club. Under the literal reading the dry run drops Center Stage's and Reggies' rooms on §1, as it does The
  EARL. Outback Presents (Center Stage; written 'Outback' at Beachland) is not counted as AEG: AEG's reported stake in it is a minority one.
  The dry run's literal reading (`results/backtest2-dryrun.json`, "literal_reading") drops 25 rooms on §1: the
  rooms of Center Stage (4), Reggies (4), Arts at the Armory (4) and Trinity Centre (5), and The EARL, The Louisiana, Pumpehuset,
  Brudenell Social Club, The Castle Hotel, Le Ministère, Rickshaw Theatre and Rio Theatre; and 4 on §5: 529, The Wardrobe, Turbo
  Haus and The Pearl. It leaves 20 clusters, with 6 clusters (10 test rooms) capped after them, as `candidates.md` gives it; the
  §1 list is longer than the scouting estimate in the first bullet of this section. Its joins ("merged") also differ from that
  estimate: it joins Bitterzoet with Festsaal Kreuzberg, Privatclub and Urban Spree (9 shared presenters), Cactus Club with MilkBoy
  Philadelphia and Blue Strawberry (1), and Bottom of the Hill with Make-Out Room (1), and it joins neither Cinetol nor Tolhuistuin
  with Bitterzoet. Off Broadway is on none of these lists.

## Notes for compiling
- The eight buildings that entered the cap on 17-18 Oct (`chi-greenmill`, `chi-martyrs`, `chi-reggies-*`, `chi-oldtown-*`,
  `cle-beachland-*`, `cle-musicbox-*`, `atl-centerstage-*`, `cph-amagerbio-*`): the Coverage lines of their sections give settle or
  re-admission counts; their 'Coverage, compiled' and 'Presenters, compiled' lines give the compiled counts of 18 Oct 2026
  (one compiled list per building). Merged into `shows.csv`
  (9,304 rows since the Off Broadway rows were added, 18 Oct 23:02 server time; 9,250 before), every one of their roles agrees
  with `shows.csv` (Amendment 12). The dry run on it (`results/backtest2-dryrun.json`) gives 20
  clusters, 26 test rooms and 1,578 S2 test rows (1,539 live), no coverage problem (`stl-offbroadway` now has its 54 compiled rows,
  capped), no room whose majority presenter is not its operator, and 20 clusters under the literal reading (25 rooms dropped on §1,
  4 on §5, 6 clusters with 10 test rooms capped, as in the section above). Room counts: the "rooms" list of `results/backtest2-dryrun.json`.
- Scope: a venue's calendar source is all of its own event listings, not only a music category. Non-music rows are removed only by the
  Amendment 4 words; rows that pass them (comedy without the word, talks, screenings) count, as the text says.
- Roles at the line: Pumpehuset (20 / 20) and Le Ministère (S2 21) are test rooms, and the rooms of VEGA are pool, on pre-compile
  counts. Tranzac's four named rooms, Festsaal Kreuzberg's Festsaal and Cinetol's Zaal became test rooms on the compiled lists (17 Oct). Badehaus (S2), The Castle Hotel and Night & Day Cafe were counted partly on advance
  listings. Amendment 12 checks every role against `shows.csv`; a role that disagrees is corrected in `venues.csv` with the
  reason here, before any Qloo call.
- More test rooms at or near the line: Bitterzoet (S2 >=20, a lower bound), The Pabst Theater (S1 20; S2 23 run units, 9 of them
  on the thumbnail code alone), Loppen (S2 21) and Kings (S1 >=21, margin 1). The Burren back room (S2 >=22 on one capture, 4 of them 'TBD' rows) is no longer near
  the line: its compiled rows give 135 / 114 (as its section says).
  OCCII is S2 26 under the literal rule but 19 read by meaning. The Burren front room became a test room on the compiled rows
  (131 / 112; Amendment 1).
- House rooms: the Tolhuistuin house room (85 / 45) and the Trinity Centre house room (47 / 32; 49 / 34 compiled) reach 20 / 20 but hold the rows with no room
  given, so they are pool by Amendment 14; the runner's coverage check exempts the room named after its building.
- Rooms: VEGA rows go to the room their venue.name gives; a row whose venue.name names no single room (just "VEGA") goes to
  `cph-vega-house`, a pool room of the same building. A row given two rooms goes to the first one listed.
- Rooms: Tranzac rows go to the room its event page's rooms[] gives, Festsaal Kreuzberg rows to the room its location field gives;
  rows with no room go to the house room (`tor-tranzac-house`, `ber-festsaal-house`); rows whose new_location names another building
  are `off-site`.
- Titles: 529's feed title is empty on many rows; the title is then the headliner names its calendar shows (from the band ids), never
  empty. Make-Out Room posts are titled with the date; each event is a bold name in the post, and lines that are handles, links, prices,
  the venue's name or award notes are not events.
- The Rockwell: take the canceled flag from the site's list view (schema.org eventStatus), not from the feed; two S2 rows are canceled
  only there.
- Lizard Lounge (not used since 17 Oct; see `bos-lizard` above): "No Event Tonight" and "Closed" rows are all-day events whose feed start reads 02:00; they are notices that no show takes place,
  so they are not compiled into `shows.csv`. No other all-day row may be read as a 02:00 start: a date-only value keeps its listed date
  (Amendment 3).
- VEGA: VEGA Beyond pages are a separate series that may be held elsewhere; rows whose venue.name is not a VEGA room are dropped as
  `off-site`. Two guided tours ("Rundvisning") were left out of the coverage count; no registered rule drops them in the run.
- Badehaus: the weekly pub quiz is removed by the Amendment 4 word "quiz"; two acts listed under two slugs on one night (15 Aug,
  9 Sep) are one row each, as in the count.
- Rickshaw Theatre: the feed has no venue field, so off-site rows are found only on the show's own page; a row whose page names another
  building is `off-site`.
- Horseshoe Tavern and The Pearl: the live sites keep only coming shows, so rows come from the union of web.archive.org copies of their
  own pages, keyed on date and title or slug; a row that vanished from a later copy before its date is left out.
- Bottom of the Hill: one title on 6 Apr carries price text; titles are cut before the price.
- Capacities (Amendment 8): Badehaus 450, Festsaal 1,100 and The Castle Hotel 80 are the figures the venues publish.
  Lizard Lounge's 100 (not used since 17 Oct) comes from its private-event page ("from 20 - 100"), the only figure it publishes.
  The Rockwell 203 (https://therockwell.org/our-space/, 'for a max capacity of 203'), Bottom of the Hill 246
  (https://www.bottomofthehill.com/faq.html, 'Our capacity is 246.') and The Wardrobe 500 (https://www.thewardrobe.co.uk/about/,
  'Downstairs homes a 500-capacity renowned venue and events space') are from the venues' own pages, as quoted in the 15 Oct
  eligibility record.
